Always stores 1 in the active-filter word at offset 0x64c of the context block that the state-block pointer at offset 0xcf4 selects. Touches no hardware. |
| `d_convolution_2d` | 0x0c036b9c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x36b9c). Disable handler for two-dimensional convolution. Clears the state-block byte at offset 0x785 and bit 0x40 of the pixel-path flag word at offset 0xc88; if the separable-filter bit 0x80 is still set it stores 2 in the active-filter word at offset 0x64c of the context block named by the pointer at offset 0xcf4, so the separable filter takes over. Touches no hardware. |
| `e_convolution_sep2d` | 0x0c036bc4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x36bc4). Enable handler for the separable two-dimensional convolution. Sets the state-block byte at offset 0x786; if pixel transform is enabled and the transform-mode word is 0x81c5, ORs bit 0x80 into the pixel-path flag word at offset 0xc88. When the two-dimensional bit 0x40 is clear, stores 2 in the active-filter word at offset 0x64c of the context block named by the pointer at offset 0xcf4. Touches no hardware. |
| `d_convolution_sep2d` | 0x0c036c08 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x36c08). Disable handler for the separable two-dimensional convolution. Clears the state-block byte at offset 0x786 and bit 0x80 of the pixel-path flag word at offset 0xc88, then returns. Leaves the active-filter word alone and touches no hardware. |
| `e_pixel_transform` | 0x0c037630 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x37630). Enable handler for the pixel-transform stage. Sets the state-block byte at offset 0x796, then, if the transform-mode word at offset 0x36c is not 0x81c5, ORs bit 0x80000 into the pixel-path flag word at offset 0xc88; if it is 0x81c5, instead re-applies the three convolution bits for each convolution whose enable byte (offsets 0x784-0x786) is set. Touches no hardware. |
| `d_pixel_transform` | 0x0c037694 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x37694). Disable handler for the pixel-transform stage. Clears the state-block byte at offset 0x796, then clears the three convolution bits and bit 0x80000 of the pixel-path flag word at offset 0xc88, turning every convolution path off at once. Touches no hardware. |

## GL state handlers

Token handlers and helpers that keep GL state (colour, lighting, material, fog, blending, depth, stencil, hints, evaluators) in the state block and send it to the engines or raster boards.

| Symbol | Address | What it is and does |
|---|---|---|
| `arm_alphafunc` | 0x0c012bbc | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x12bbc). Token handler 0xca for the alpha-test function. It reads the function and the reference value, rejects functions outside 0x200-0x207 through the invalid-enum error, stores them at state-block offsets 0x798 and 0x79c, sends the function to port 0x474 and the reference to port 0x840 with the alpha-function engine handler address in the low half of the word at 0xfc, and returns to the token dispatcher. |
| `arm_clearindex` | 0x0c012bf4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x12bf4). Token handler 0x1c for the colour-index clear value. It reads one payload word and stores it at state-block offset 0x7d4; it sends nothing to the engines or raster boards. |
| `arm_clearcolor` | 0x0c012c00 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x12c00). Token handler 0x1d for the clear colour. It reads four payload words and stores them in order at state-block offsets 0x7c4-0x7d0 through a temporary stack copy; it sends nothing to the hardware, so the colour is used later by the clear code. |
| `arm_colormask` | 0x0c012c3c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x12c3c). Token handler 0x21 for the colour write mask. It reads four per-channel bytes, stores them at state-block offsets 0x7de-0x7e1, builds a 16-bit channel mask that it stores in the context-memory word at offset 0x6cc, and in colour-index mode stops there. Otherwise it writes a 0 to register 0x1408 and then, through descriptor 0x58404800, the channel enables to address word 0x01341c00, blend and logic-op bits to 0x01361c03 and 0x013c1c0d, and a synchronising write to 0x01681408. It also updates the mask-all flags at offset 0xcf0. |
| `arm_indexmask` | 0x0c012e1c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x12e1c). Token handler 0x23 for the colour-index write mask. It reads the mask, ANDs it with the legal index-bit mask kept at state-block offsets 0x7dc-0x7dd, stores the result at 0x7d8, and in colour-index mode sets or clears bit 1 of the flag byte at 0xcf0 according to whether the mask is full. It then shifts the mask by the index width and writes it, copied into two fields 12 bits apart, to raster address word 0x013d1c14 through descriptor 0x584fc800. |
| `arm_blendcolor` | 0x0c012ee4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x12ee4). Token handler 0xe6 for the blend constant colour. It reads four payload words and stores them at state-block offsets 0x7a0-0x7ac. In colour mode (not colour-index) it also writes 0 to register 0x1408, sends the four words to the engines (the first to port 0x474, the rest to port 0x840) with the user-colour handler, and writes 0 to raster address word 0x01681408 through descriptor 0x58404800. In colour-index mode it only stores them. |
| `arm_blendequation` | 0x0c012f9c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x12f9c). Token handler 0xe7 for the blend equation. It accepts only the enums 0x8006-0x800b except 0x8009, plus 0xbf1, reporting invalid-enum otherwise. It stores the enum at state-block offset 0x7bc and, in colour mode, writes a packed control word (an equation number derived from the enum, factor fields from the bytes at 0x7b8-0x7b9, and a logic-op bit) to raster address word 0x013c1c0d through descriptor 0x584fc800. |
| `color_init` | 0x0c014084 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x14084). Context start-up routine for the colour state, called once by the context initialiser. It writes 0 to register 0x1408, then sets the colour shadow to defaults: alpha function 0x207, the blend constants at state-block offsets 0x7b0-0x7b4, blend bytes at 0x7b8-0x7b9, blend equation 0x8006, all four colour-mask bytes and the dither flag. It writes matching defaults to the raster boards through descriptor 0x58404800 (three address words, the first 0x013c1c0d) and ends with a synchronising write to 0x01681408. |
| `color_pop` | 0x0c014250 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x14250). Restores the colour attribute group when attributes are popped (called by the pop handler). It replays the saved alpha-test flag and function, the colour mask or index mask, the logic-op and blend state, blend constant colour and the draw-buffer selection from the state block into the engines and raster boards, ending with `resolve_banks` and a synchronising write to address word 0x01681408. |
| `arm_cleardepth` | 0x0c01465c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x1465c). Token handler 0x1f for the depth clear value. It reads two payload words and stores them at state-block offsets 0x6e4-0x6e8. If the depth width is zero it returns. For a depth width of 0x20 it sends the words and a write mask to the engines with the clear-depth handler; otherwise it converts the double to the narrower packed form and writes it to raster address word 0x013a1c09 through descriptor 0x584fc800. |
| `arm_depthfunc` | 0x0c0147a0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x147a0). Token handler 0xce for the depth comparison. It reads the function enum, stores it at state-block offset 0x6dc, and writes it shifted left 13 bits, with mask 0xe000, to raster address word 0x01391c08 through descriptor 0x584fc800. It does no range check on the enum. |
| `arm_depthmask` | 0x0c0147d0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x147d0). Token handler 0x22 for the depth write mask. It reads the flag, stores its low byte at state-block offset 0x6e0, and writes it shifted left 16 bits, with mask 0x10000, to raster address word 0x01391c08 through descriptor 0x584fc800. |
| `arm_clearstencil` | 0x0c0148b8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x148b8). Token handler 0x1e for the stencil clear value. It reads one word and stores it at state-block offset 0x70c. When the stencil width is exactly 1 it also writes the value to raster address word 0x013a1c09 through descriptor 0x584fc800, shifted left 16 unless the depth width is 0x20. It then returns to the token dispatcher. |
| `arm_stencilmask` | 0x0c014904 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x14904). Token handler 0x20 for the stencil write mask. It reads one word, stores it at state-block offset 0x718, writes it shifted left 21 bits (mask 0x1fe00000) to raster address word 0x01381c07 through descriptor 0x584fc800, and sets or clears bit 2 of the flag byte at 0xcf0 according to whether the mask covers every stencil bit. |
| `arm_stencilfunc` | 0x0c014960 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x14960). Token handler 0xcc for the stencil comparison. It reads function, reference and mask words, stores them at state-block offsets 0x6fc and 0x710-0x714, and writes one packed word (reference shifted 5, function shifted 2, mask shifted 13, each a byte) with mask 0x1ffffc to raster address word 0x01381c07 through descriptor 0x584fc800. |
| `arm_stencilop` | 0x0c0149b8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x149b8). Token handler 0xcd for the stencil operations. It reads three operation enums, stores them at state-block offsets 0x700-0x708, maps enum 0 to 4 and 0x150a to 5, packs the three into 3-bit fields, and writes the result under mask 0x1ff to raster address word 0x01391c08 through descriptor 0x584fc800. Other enums go in unchecked; the mask keeps only their low bits. |
| `depth_init` | 0x0c014a9c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x14a9c). Context start-up routine for depth and stencil state, called once by the context initialiser. It stores defaults in the state block: 0x3ff00000 as the high word of 1.0 at two places, depth function 0x201, write mask 1, stencil function 0x207 and all three stencil operations 0x1e00. It sends 1.0 (0x3f800000) to the engines with the depth-range handler, and writes matching raster defaults to address words 0x01391c08 and 0x01381c07 through descriptor 0x584fc800. |
| `stencil_pop` | 0x0c014b34 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x14b34). Restores the stencil attribute group when attributes are popped (called by the pop handler). It writes 0 to register 0x1408, re-sends the saved stencil function, reference, mask and operations to raster address words 0x01381c07 and 0x01391c08 through descriptor 0x58404800, recomputes bit 2 of the flag byte at state-block offset 0xcf0, rewrites the clear value when the stencil width is 1, and ends with a synchronising write to 0x01681408. |
| `depth_pop` | 0x0c014ca0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x14ca0). Restores the depth attribute group when attributes are popped (called by the pop handler). It rewrites the depth function, write mask and test flag to raster address word 0x01391c08 through descriptor 0x584fc800 and, if a depth buffer exists, the saved clear value either through the clear-depth engine handler or as a packed value to address word 0x013a1c09. |
| `arm_blendfunc` | 0x0c015768 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x15768). Token 0x0cb handler. Keeps the source and destination blend factors (state block offsets 0x7b0-0x7b4), maps each graphics-library factor enum to a four-bit hardware code through a chain of range tests, and stores the codes as bytes at offsets 0x7b8-0x7b9. Unless the word at offset 0x7bc is 0x8007-0x8008 (the minimum and maximum equations) it writes the packed codes to raster register 0x013c1c0d through descriptor 0x584fc800. |
| `arm_logicop` | 0x0c015c18 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x15c18). Token 0x02e handler. Stores the logic-operation code from the payload at state block offset 0x7c0 and writes it doubled to raster register 0x013c1c0d through descriptor 0x584fc800 (select word 0x1e). |
| `arm_edgeflag` | 0x0c015c7c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x15c7c). Token 0x0a7 handler. Writes the edge flag to descriptor 0x52000020 as all ones when the payload is nonzero and as zero otherwise; keeps no copy in the state block. |
| `arm_hint` | 0x0c016468 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x16468). Token 0x0bb handler. Takes a hint target (0xc50-0xc54) and a mode from the payload and stores the mode in one of five words at state block offsets 0x7ec-0x7fc. The point hint (offset 0x7f0) also re-derives points with `ResolvePoint` and the line hint (offset 0x7f4) re-derives lines with `ResolveLine`. Any other target records an invalid-enum error. |
| `color` | 0x0c0164d8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x164d8). Sends a current colour to the engines. Takes four floating-point values in the first four arguments and writes the first three to the data port at 0x840 and the fourth to port 0x8c0 after descriptor 0xd8685000 has been written to the descriptor port. Called once, by `prim_init`, to load the default colour. |
| `cindex` | 0x0c0164f8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x164f8). Sends a current colour index to the engines. Writes descriptor 0xd868d800 to the descriptor port and the first argument to port 0x8c0. Called once, by `prim_init`, to load the default index. |
| `vertex` | 0x0c01650c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x1650c). Sends a vertex to the engines. Writes descriptor 0xd8674000 to the descriptor port, the first three arguments to port 0x840 and the fourth to port 0x8c0. No call to it appears anywhere in the code. |
| `normal` | 0x0c01652c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x1652c). Sends a current normal to the engines. Writes descriptor 0xd867da00 to the descriptor port, the first two arguments to port 0x840 and the third to port 0x8c0. Called once, by `prim_init`, to load the default normal. |
| `texcoord` | 0x0c016548 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x16548). Sends a current texture coordinate to the engines. Writes descriptor 0xd8696000 to the descriptor port, the first three arguments to port 0x840 and the fourth to port 0x8c0. Called once, by `prim_init`, to load the default coordinate. |
| `edgeflag` | 0x0c016568 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x16568). Sends the current edge flag to the engines. Writes descriptor 0x52000020 to the descriptor port, then the low byte of the first argument negated (so 1 becomes all ones) to port 0x8c0. Called once, by `prim_init`. |
| `arm_irisgl_mode` | 0x0c016770 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x16770). Token 0x112 handler. Sets or clears a mode flag in the per-context record (word 0x111c, found through the state block word at 0xcf4) and bit 0 of its flag word at 0x1114, writes zero to two mode descriptors, starts `mgeb_SetLMCFlag` or `mgeb_ClearLMCFlag`, writes the flag word to descriptor 0x52800020 and jumps to the transfer-mask routine. |
| `arm_tmeshmode` | 0x0c016854 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x16854). Token 0x1c9 handler. Sets or clears bit 0x20 of the flag word at offset 0x1114 in the per-context record (found through the state block word at 0xcf4) according to whether the payload is nonzero, then writes the flag word to descriptor 0x52800020. |
| `arm_lmc_color` | 0x0c016898 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x16898). Token 0x113 handler. Stores the payload word at offset 0x1120 of the per-context record (found through the state block word at 0xcf4) and jumps to the transfer-mask routine so the distributor mask follows the new value. |
| `arm_shademodel` | 0x0c016ab8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x16ab8). Token 0x0c0 handler. Checks that the payload is flat (0x1d00) or smooth (0x1d01), otherwise records an invalid-enum error. Stores it at state block offset 0x378, passes it through port 0x47c and starts the engine handler `mgebi_ShadeModel`. |
| `arm_lightmodelf` | 0x0c016ae4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x16ae4). Token 0x009 handler. Reads the parameter name from the payload and, for the three light-model parameters 0xb51-0xb53, jumps through the table `lightmodelf` to the routine for that parameter. For any other name it discards the payload and records an invalid-enum error. |
| `arm_lightmodeli` | 0x0c016b20 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x16b20). Token 0x00a handler. Reads the parameter name from the payload and, for the three light-model parameters 0xb51-0xb53, jumps through the table `lightmodeli` to the routine for that parameter. For any other name it discards the payload and records an invalid-enum error. |
| `lightmodel_locv` | 0x0c016b5c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x16b5c). Local-viewer entry of the light-model tables (both float and integer). Reads the four payload words, stores nonzero-ness of the first at state block offset 0x38c, passes the first word through port 0x478 and starts the engine handler `mgeb_LightModelLocV`. |
| `lightmodel_2side` | 0x0c016b90 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x16b90). Two-sided entry of the light-model tables (both float and integer). Reads the four payload words, stores nonzero-ness of the first at state block offset 0x38d, passes the first word through port 0x478 and starts the engine handler `mgeb_LightModel2Side`. |
| `lightmodel_gamb` | 0x0c016bc4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x16bc4). Sets the global ambient light from four floating-point arguments. Stores all four at state block offsets 0x37c-0x388, sends the first through port 0x478 and the next two through port 0x840 (the fourth is kept but not sent) and starts the engine handler `mgeb_LightModelGAmb`. |
| `lightmodelf_gamb` | 0x0c016bf0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x16bf0). Float entry of the `lightmodelf` table for the global ambient parameter. Reads the four payload words into the first four arguments and jumps to `lightmodel_gamb`. |
| `lightmodeli_gamb` | 0x0c016c04 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x16c04). Integer entry of the integer light-model table for the global ambient parameter. Reads the four payload words, converts each from signed fixed point to float with `convFixToFloat` and jumps to `lightmodel_gamb`. |
| `arm_colormaterial` | 0x0c016c70 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x16c70). Token 0x0b5 handler. Reads a face and a parameter from the payload, records an invalid-enum error unless the parameter is one of the five material parameters (ambient, diffuse, specular, emission, ambient and diffuse), then jumps through the table `colormaterial` for front (0x404) or back (0x405) and to `colormaterial_frontback` for both (0x408). |
| `colormaterial_front` | 0x0c016ce4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x16ce4). Colour-material entry for the front face. Records face 0x404 and the parameter at state block offsets 0x370-0x374, rewrites the distributor transfer mask twice with `set_ged_xfer_mask` around two descriptor writes, and starts `mgeb_LiClrMtrlFront` with the parameter at port 0x478. |
| `colormaterial_back` | 0x0c016d7c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x16d7c). Colour-material entry for the back face. Records face 0x405 and the parameter at state block offsets 0x370-0x374, rewrites the distributor transfer mask twice with `set_ged_xfer_mask` around two descriptor writes, and starts `mgeb_LiClrMtrlBack` with the parameter at port 0x478. |
| `colormaterial_frontback` | 0x0c016e08 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x16e08). Colour-material entry for both faces. Records face 0x408 and the parameter at state block offsets 0x370-0x374, rewrites the distributor transfer mask twice with `set_ged_xfer_mask` around two descriptor writes, and starts `mgeb_LiClrMtrlFrontBack` with the parameter at port 0x478. |
| `arm_lightf` | 0x0c016e94 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x16e94). Token 0x007 handler. Reads the light (0x4000-0x4007) and the parameter (0x1200-0x1209) from the payload and, when both are valid, jumps through the table `lightfi` to the per-parameter routine with the light number as the first argument and 0 (float form) as the second. Otherwise it discards the payload and calls `set_invalid_enum`. |
| `arm_lighti` | 0x0c016ee8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x16ee8). Token 0x008 handler (integer glLight): reads the light number and parameter name, rejects a light outside 0-7 or a name outside 0x1200-0x1209 by draining the payload and calling `set_invalid_enum`, otherwise jumps through the light-parameter table indexed by name minus 0x1200 with the integer-form flag set. |
| `light_amb` | 0x0c016f38 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x16f38). Light-parameter routine for the ambient colour of a light, taken from the light-parameter dispatch table, reached from the integer and float light token handlers with the light number as the first argument and a nonzero second argument for integer form. Reads four payload words, converts them with `convFixToFloat` when integer form is set, stores them in the light's block of the state block at offset 0x398 plus 0x64 per light, then sends the light's engine-memory address to port 0x478, the four words to port 0x840 and the light-ambient engine handler to port 0x8c0. |
| `light_diff` | 0x0c016ffc | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x16ffc). Light-parameter routine for the diffuse colour of a light, taken from the light-parameter dispatch table, reached from the integer and float light token handlers with the light number as the first argument and a nonzero second argument for integer form. Reads four payload words, converts them with `convFixToFloat` when integer form is set, stores them in the light's block of the state block at offset 0x3a8 plus 0x64 per light, then sends the light's engine-memory address to port 0x478, the four words to port 0x840 and the light-diffuse engine handler to port 0x8c0. |
| `light_spec` | 0x0c0170b8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x170b8). Light-parameter routine for the specular colour of a light, taken from the light-parameter dispatch table, reached from the integer and float light token handlers with the light number as the first argument and a nonzero second argument for integer form. Reads four payload words, converts them with `convFixToFloat` when integer form is set, stores them in the light's block of the state block at offset 0x3b8 plus 0x64 per light, then sends the light's engine-memory address to port 0x478, the four words to port 0x840 and the light-specular engine handler to port 0x8c0. |
| `light_pos` | 0x0c017174 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x17174). Light-parameter routine for the position of a light, taken from the light-parameter dispatch table, reached from the integer and float light token handlers with the light number as the first argument and a nonzero second argument for integer form. Reads four payload words, converts integers without colour scaling when integer form is set, stores them at offset 0x3c8 plus 0x64 per light in the state block, then sends the light's engine-memory address to port 0x478, the four words to port 0x840 and the light-position engine handler to port 0x8c0. |
| `light_sdir` | 0x0c017234 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x17234). Light-parameter routine for the spot direction of a light, taken from the light-parameter dispatch table, reached from the integer and float light token handlers with the light number as the first argument and a nonzero second argument for integer form. Reads three payload words and discards the fourth, converts integers without colour scaling when integer form is set, stores them at offset 0x3d8 plus 0x64 per light in the state block, then sends the light's engine-memory address to port 0x478, the three words to port 0x840 and the spot-direction engine handler to port 0x8c0. |
| `light_sexp` | 0x0c0172d4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x172d4). Light-parameter routine for the spot exponent of a light, taken from the light-parameter dispatch table, reached from the integer and float light token handlers with the light number as the first argument and a nonzero second argument for integer form. Reads one value and discards the other payload words, converts an integer to a single float when integer form is set, stores it at offset 0x3e8 plus 0x64 per light in the state block, then sends the light's engine-memory address to port 0x478, the value and then the light number to port 0x840 and the spot-exponent engine handler to port 0x8c0. |
| `light_scut` | 0x0c01733c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x1733c). Light-parameter routine for the spot cutoff angle of a light, taken from the light-parameter dispatch table, reached from the integer and float light token handlers with the light number as the first argument and a nonzero second argument for integer form. Reads one value and discards the other payload words, converts an integer to a single float when integer form is set, stores it at offset 0x3ec plus 0x64 per light in the state block, then sends the light's engine-memory address to port 0x478, the value to port 0x840 and the spot-cutoff engine handler to port 0x8c0. |
| `light_k0` | 0x0c0173a0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x173a0). Light-parameter routine for the constant attenuation of a light, taken from the light-parameter dispatch table, reached from the integer and float light token handlers with the light number as the first argument and a nonzero second argument for integer form. Reads one value and discards the other payload words, rejects a value with the sign bit set through `set_invalid_value`, converts an integer to a single float when integer form is set, stores it at offset 0x3f0 plus 0x64 per light in the state block, then sends the light's engine-memory address to port 0x478, the value to port 0x840 and the constant-attenuation engine handler to port 0x8c0. |
| `light_k1` | 0x0c017410 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x17410). Light-parameter routine for the linear attenuation of a light, taken from the light-parameter dispatch table, reached from the integer and float light token handlers with the light number as the first argument and a nonzero second argument for integer form. Reads one value and discards the other payload words, rejects a value with the sign bit set through `set_invalid_value`, converts an integer to a single float when integer form is set, stores it at offset 0x3f4 plus 0x64 per light in the state block, then sends the light's engine-memory address to port 0x478, the value to port 0x840 and the linear-attenuation engine handler to port 0x8c0. |
| `light_k2` | 0x0c01747c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x1747c). Light-parameter routine for the quadratic attenuation of a light, taken from the light-parameter dispatch table, reached from the integer and float light token handlers with the light number as the first argument and a nonzero second argument for integer form. Reads one value and discards the other payload words, rejects a value with the sign bit set through `set_invalid_value`, converts an integer to a single float when integer form is set, stores it at offset 0x3f8 plus 0x64 per light in the state block, then sends the light's engine-memory address to port 0x478, the value to port 0x840 and the quadratic-attenuation engine handler to port 0x8c0. |
| `ReCompPwrTable` | 0x0c017b3c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x17b3c). Sends the engine handler that recomputes the lighting specular power table to port 0x4f8 (all engines) and returns. Its only caller is `activate_private`, which runs it after restoring a context's transform and fog state. |
| `doFixToFloat` | 0x0c017b4c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x17b4c). Converts a 32-bit magnitude to IEEE single-precision bits: the first argument selects a plain integer (zero) or a fraction below 1.0 (nonzero), the second is the magnitude and the third a sign flag. Finds the leading one, builds the exponent and truncates the mantissa without rounding, returns 0 for a zero magnitude, and sets the sign bit from the third argument. |
| `scaleFix` | 0x0c017bb4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x17bb4). Returns the magnitude of twice the signed first argument plus one and stores a sign flag (1 when the argument is negative) through the pointer in the second argument. **Inferred:** this is GL's mapping of an integer colour component c to (2c+1)/(2^32-1), because `convFixToFloat` feeds the result to the fraction form of the integer-to-float converter. |
| `convFixToFloat` | 0x0c017bf4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x17bf4). Converts a signed 32-bit integer (second argument) to single-precision bits: when the first argument is nonzero it scales through `scaleFix` to a fraction in 0-1 as GL does for integer colours, otherwise it takes the plain absolute value and sign; either way it finishes in the integer-to-float converter and returns the float. Called by the light, light-model and material parameter routines for integer-form tokens. |
| `light_init` | 0x0c017c54 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x17c54). Sets the lighting defaults in the state block at context creation: lighting and colour-material flags off, smooth shading (0x1d01), colour-material face 0x408 and mode 0x1602, global ambient 0.2 with alpha 1.0, and for each of eight lights a spot direction pointing down the negative z axis, cutoff 180.0, attenuation 1.0 and alpha 1.0, with light 0 white. It also sends one short command through descriptor 0x58404800 to the engines. |
| `arm_selectbuffer` | 0x0c0265a4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x265a4). Token handler 0x018: a single branch to the bad-command routine, which prints the token numbers, reads and prints 100 payload words and then spins; the firmware implements no real handler for this token. |
| `arm_subdiv_patch_parameter` | 0x0c03fef0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x3fef0). Token handler 0x1d3: reads a parameter name and value, sends them to the first and second operand ports and starts the engine handler `mgeb_Subdiv`, so the engines set one subdivision-patch parameter. |
| `arm_subdiv_patch_setup` | 0x0c03ff14 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x3ff14). Token handler 0x1d1: maps the patch type in its last payload word (types from 0x2a21 up to 0x2a29) to a distributor transfer mask with the low three, five or seven bits set, or zero for any other type, writes the 0x12121212 filler through a register-write descriptor and loads the mask through descriptor 0x55c00020, the distributor transfer-mask register. |
| `arm_subdiv_patch_cleanup` | 0x0c03ff7c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x3ff7c). Token handler 0x1d2: discards its payload word and restores the normal distributor transfer mask from state-block offset 0xc90 through descriptor 0x55c00020, undoing the patch set-up. |
| `arm_subdiv_patch` | 0x0c03ffac | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x3ffac). Token handler 0x1d0: selects one engine (port 0x430) and forwards a patch header, then either a short form (when bit 31 of the first word is set) or fourteen more words, and starts the engine handler `mges_SubdivTag`, sending the patch to the engines to subdivide. |
| `expX` | 0x04051804 | **Established** (`arm1.u` 6.5.30m). 128 decreasing floats from 1.0 (the next is 0.9394) loaded into engine memory by `InitFogGlobal`. **Inferred:** coarse steps of the exponential fog function, multiplied with the fine table. |
| `expY` | 0x04051a04 | **Established** (`arm1.u` 6.5.30m). 258 floats falling slowly from 1.0 (0.99976 ...) loaded by `InitFogGlobal`. **Inferred:** fine steps of the exponential fog function between the coarse entries. |

## Transform, matrices and clipping

Matrix modes and operations, viewport, clip planes and the transform attribute paths ([arm1_firmware.md](arm1_firmware.md#matrix-tokens-flip-between-hardware-and-arm)).

| Symbol | Address | What it is and does |
|---|---|---|
| `arm_depthrange` | 0x0c014800 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x14800). Token handler 0xcf for the depth range. It reads four payload words and sends the first three to the data port at 0x840 and the fourth to port 0x8c0 after the distributor descriptor 0x78601000, writes the depth-range engine handler address to port 0x4f8, and stores the four words in the state block at offsets 0x72c-0x738. |
| `arm_clipplane` | 0x0c018cc0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x18cc0). Token 0x0b4 handler (glClipPlane): reads nine payload words, sends the first through port 0x8c0 after selecting all engines (port 0x428), the next seven through descriptor 0x78601000 and port 0x840 and the last through port 0x8c0, then starts the engine user-clip-plane handler through port 0x4f8. **Inferred:** the words are the plane number and the four double-precision plane coefficients, from the GL call's signature. |
| `matrix_passthru` | 0x0c018ef0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x18ef0). Restores the original hardware routing of the matrix tokens 0x0d1-0x0df: sets the map-access bit in the control register at 0x200050, copies fifteen entries from the static token list (offset taken from the saved list index) into the token map at 0x400344, then puts the register back. Called when the matrix mode leaves the colour matrix, and by `xform_init` and `xform_restore`. |
| `matrix_vector` | 0x0c018f44 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x18f44). Diverts the matrix tokens 0x0d1-0x0df to the ARM for the colour matrix: sets the map-access bit in the control register at 0x200050, writes the addresses of the fifteen colour-matrix handlers (each shifted left four) into the token map at 0x400344-0x40037c, then restores the register. Called when the matrix mode becomes the colour matrix and by `xform_restore`. |
| `arm_matrixmode` | 0x0c01904c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x1904c). Token 0x0d0 handler (matrix-mode call): does nothing if the mode is unchanged, calls `matrix_passthru` when leaving the colour mode, stores the mode, sends it to port 0x478 with the matrix-mode engine handler, then for modelview, projection or texture loads the ten matrix-operation engine handlers of that mode into the distributor's handler table through descriptors 0x46000020 (index 3) and 0x46200020; for the colour mode it ends in the token-diversion routine. |
| `arm_frustum` | 0x0c019300 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x19300). Colour-matrix version of the frustum call (token 0x0d1), installed in the token map by the matrix diversion routine: forwards the twelve payload words through descriptor 0x78601000 and port 0x840 (last word through port 0x8c0), starts the colour-matrix frustum engine handler through port 0x4f8, then calls `invalidate_colormatrix` so the ARM's tracked colour matrix is dropped. |
| `arm_loadidentity` | 0x0c01939c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x1939c). Colour-matrix version of the load-identity call (token 0x0d2), installed in the token map by the matrix diversion routine: sends the final payload word to port 0x478 and the colour-matrix load-identity engine handler to port 0x8c0, then jumps to `update_colormatrix` with the bit pattern 0x8421 that marks the four diagonal elements as 1.0. |
| `arm_loadmatrixf` | 0x0c0193c0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x193c0). Colour-matrix version of the float load-matrix call (token 0x0d3), installed in the token map by the matrix diversion routine: selects all engines, forwards the sixteen floats to port 0x840 and the colour-matrix load handler to port 0x8c0, and records which elements are exactly one or exactly zero; if every element is one or the other it calls `update_colormatrix` with the mask of ones, otherwise `invalidate_colormatrix`. |
| `arm_loadmatrixd` | 0x0c019478 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x19478). Colour-matrix version of the double load-matrix call (token 0x0d4), installed in the token map by the matrix diversion routine: sends descriptor 0x78601000, forwards thirty-two payload words to port 0x840 and the final word to port 0x8c0, starts the colour-matrix load engine handler through port 0x4f8, then jumps to `invalidate_colormatrix`. |
| `arm_multmatrixf` | 0x0c0194b4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x194b4). Colour-matrix version of the float multiply-matrix call (token 0x0d5), installed in the token map by the matrix diversion routine: selects all engines, forwards the sixteen floats to port 0x840 and the colour-matrix multiply engine handler to port 0x8c0, then jumps to `invalidate_colormatrix`. |
| `arm_multmatrixd` | 0x0c0194e8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x194e8). Colour-matrix version of the double multiply-matrix call (token 0x0d6), installed in the token map by the matrix diversion routine: sends descriptor 0x78601000, forwards thirty-two payload words to port 0x840 and the final word to port 0x8c0, starts the colour-matrix multiply engine handler through port 0x4f8, then jumps to `invalidate_colormatrix`. |
| `arm_ortho` | 0x0c019520 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x19520). Colour-matrix version of the orthographic-projection call (token 0x0d7), installed in the token map by the matrix diversion routine: forwards the twelve payload words through descriptor 0x78601000 and port 0x840 (last word through port 0x8c0), starts the colour-matrix ortho engine handler through port 0x4f8, then calls `invalidate_colormatrix`. |
| `arm_popmatrix` | 0x0c0195bc | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x195bc). Colour-matrix version of the pop-matrix call (token 0x0d8), installed in the token map by the matrix diversion routine: sends the payload word to port 0x478 and the colour-matrix pop engine handler to port 0x8c0, then jumps to `pop_colormatrix` to pop the ARM's tracked colour-matrix stack. |
| `arm_pushmatrix` | 0x0c0195d4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x195d4). Colour-matrix version of the push-matrix call (token 0x0d9), installed in the token map by the matrix diversion routine: sends the payload word to port 0x478 and the colour-matrix push engine handler to port 0x8c0, then jumps to `push_colormatrix` to push the ARM's tracked colour-matrix stack. |
| `arm_rotated` | 0x0c0195ec | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x195ec). Colour-matrix version of the double rotate call (token 0x0da), installed in the token map by the matrix diversion routine: forwards the eight payload words through descriptor 0x78601000 and port 0x840 (last word through port 0x8c0), starts the colour-matrix rotate engine handler through port 0x4f8, then jumps to `invalidate_colormatrix`. |
| `arm_rotatef` | 0x0c01964c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x1964c). Colour-matrix version of the float rotate call (token 0x0db), installed in the token map by the matrix diversion routine: sends the angle to port 0x478, the three axis floats to port 0x840 and the colour-matrix rotate engine handler to port 0x8c0, then jumps to `invalidate_colormatrix`. |
| `arm_scaled` | 0x0c01967c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x1967c). Colour-matrix version of the double scale call (token 0x0dc), installed in the token map by the matrix diversion routine: forwards the six payload words through descriptor 0x78601000 and port 0x840 (last word through port 0x8c0), starts the colour-matrix scale engine handler through port 0x4f8, then jumps to `invalidate_colormatrix`. |
| `arm_scalef` | 0x0c0196cc | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x196cc). Colour-matrix version of the float scale call (token 0x0dd), installed in the token map by the matrix diversion routine: sends the x factor to port 0x478, the y and z factors to port 0x840 and the colour-matrix scale engine handler to port 0x8c0, then jumps to `invalidate_colormatrix`. |
| `arm_translated` | 0x0c0196f4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x196f4). Colour-matrix version of the double translate call (token 0x0de), installed in the token map by the matrix diversion routine: forwards the six payload words through descriptor 0x78601000 and port 0x840 (last word through port 0x8c0), starts the colour-matrix translate engine handler through port 0x4f8, then jumps to `invalidate_colormatrix`. |
| `arm_translatef` | 0x0c019744 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x19744). Colour-matrix version of the float translate call (token 0x0df), installed in the token map by the matrix diversion routine: sends the x offset to port 0x478, the y and z offsets to port 0x840 and the colour-matrix translate engine handler to port 0x8c0, then jumps to `invalidate_colormatrix`. |
| `xform_init` | 0x0c01976c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x1976c). Resets the engines' transform state when a context is created: sends the load-identity engine handlers for the texture, projection and modelview matrices to port 0x4f8, sets the matrix mode to modelview (0x1700) in the engines and the state block, calls `matrix_passthru`, loads the ten modelview matrix handlers into the distributor handler table through descriptors 0x46000020 (index 3) and 0x46200020, and ends by jumping to a sprite-state initialiser. |
| `xform_restore` | 0x0c019898 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x19898). Reapplies the saved matrix mode to the token map and the distributor handler table when a context is restored or transform attributes are popped: runs the colour-matrix diversion routine for the colour mode and `matrix_passthru` otherwise, then loads that mode's ten matrix handlers (modelview, projection or texture) into the engine handler table through the two descriptors 0x46000020-0x46200020. |
| `xform_pop` | 0x0c019b60 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x19b60). Re-applies transform state after an attribute pop: for each of the six user clip planes it tests that plane's enable byte in the state block (offsets 0x77d-0x782) and calls the matching enable or disable routine, does the same for the normalise enable byte at offset 0x765, then tail-branches to the transform restore routine. |

## Lines, points, polygons and raster set-up

Primitive set-up derived from GL state: line, point and polygon modes, stipple, antialiasing tables, multisampling and scissor.

| Symbol | Address | What it is and does |
|---|---|---|
| `downLoadAALineTable` | 0x0c015200 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x15200). Loads the antialiased-line table into the shared pattern memory unless it is already loaded. If the pattern-memory record at 0x04041b90 is not type 0xb20, or its two data words differ from the state-block words at offsets 0x244 and 0x7f4, it sends those words to the engines (port 0x478 and port 0x840) with the line-table download handler, then records the type and words. |
| `downLoadPolygonStipple` | 0x0c015278 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x15278). Marks the shared pattern memory as holding the polygon stipple: it sends the polygon-stipple engine handler address to port 0x4f4 and stores type 0xb42 in the pattern-memory record at 0x04041b90. It sends no stipple data and leaves the record's data words untouched. |
| `arm_frontface` | 0x0c015524 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x15524). Token 0x0ba handler. Saves the front-face winding value from the payload at state block offset 0x25c, passes it as the operand at port 0x478 and starts the engine handler `mgeb_FrontFace` (low half of the packed handler word at offset 0x64). |
| `arm_cullface` | 0x0c015544 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x15544). Token 0x0b6 handler. Saves the culled-face value from the payload at state block offset 0x258, passes it as the operand at port 0x478 and starts the engine handler `mgeb_CullFace` (low half of the packed handler word at offset 0x4c). |
| `arm_polygonmode` | 0x0c015598 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x15598). Token 0x0bf handler. Reads a face and a mode from the payload, stores the mode in the front-mode word at state block offset 0x250 for front or both faces and in the back-mode word at 0x254 for back or both. Writes 1 (line mode) or 0 to descriptor 0x49a00020, stores 0 to port 0x800 and continues into `ResolvePolygon`. |
| `arm_pointsize` | 0x0c015a00 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x15a00). Token 0x0be handler. Stores the two payload words of the point size at state block offsets 0x220-0x224, then jumps to `ResolvePoint` so the new size reaches the engines. |
| `arm_linewidth` | 0x0c015a14 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x15a14). Token 0x0bd handler. Stores the two payload words of the line width at state block offsets 0x240-0x244, then jumps to `ResolveLine` so the new width reaches the engines. |
| `arm_linestipple` | 0x0c015ca0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x15ca0). Token 0x0bc handler. Stores the repeat factor (offset 0x24c) and sixteen-bit pattern (0x248-0x249), converts the pattern into a list of start and end positions of its runs of set bits (joining a run that wraps around), and sends the repeat factor and list to all engines through port 0x840 with the line-stipple handler. A fully set pattern sends nothing. Then calls `ResolveLine`. |
| `ResolvePolygon` | 0x0c015e8c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x15e8c). Re-derives and sends how polygons are drawn. Writes raster register 0x012b0806 and a second value for multisample or polygon smoothing through descriptor 0x58404800, writes the stipple flag to register 0x012f080a, then selects all engines (port 0x438) and starts the polygon set-up handler for the render mode (select, feedback or render) with the front and back polygon modes, the stipple byte and a flag that is set when stipple and line smoothing are both on. |
| `ResolveLine` | 0x0c016024 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x16024). Re-derives and sends how lines are drawn. With line smoothing on it writes smoothing values to raster registers 0x012b0806 and 0x01330810 through descriptor 0x58404800 and sends the antialiased line table. Otherwise it chooses the line-width handler by render mode, multisample or aliased (writing raster values only in the aliased case). The width at offset 0x244 and the stipple flags go to the engines last. |
| `ResolvePoint` | 0x0c016240 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x16240). Re-derives and sends how points are drawn. Picks the point-size handler from the render mode (select, feedback or render) and, in render mode, from point smoothing (offset 0x766), multisample or aliased; sends the size at state block offset 0x224 through port 0x47c. |
| `arm_polygonoffsetext` | 0x0c016408 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x16408). Token 0x11b handler. Passes the first payload word through port 0x478 and the second through port 0x840 and starts the engine handler `mgeb_PolygonOffsetExt` (high half of the packed handler word at offset 0xc8). Keeps no copy in the state block. |
| `arm_polygonoffset` | 0x0c016428 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x16428). Token 0x168 handler. Passes the first payload word (factor) through port 0x478 and the second (units) through port 0x840 and starts the engine handler `mgeb_PolygonOffset`. Keeps no copy in the state block. |
| `initAALineTablesGlobal` | 0x0c0168ac | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x168ac). One-time startup routine called from the global initialisation list. Sends the antialiased-line table initialiser `mgeb_InitAALineTables` twice, with 1.0 at port 0x478 and table selectors 0x1101-0x1102 at port 0x840, then zeroes the pattern-memory owner word `PramContentsType` and the two data words after it. |
| `arm_viewport` | 0x0c0186ec | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x186ec). Token 0x0e3 handler (viewport call): reads x, y, width and height, rejects a negative width or height as an invalid value, clamps width and height to 0xf00, stores the four values at state-block offsets 0x71c-0x728 and jumps to `resolve_viewport`. |
| `resolve_viewport` | 0x0c01872c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x1872c). Combines the viewport rectangle at state-block offsets 0x71c-0x728 with the window origin and size fields and sends the result to all engines (port 0x438): x, y, width, height, a limit (0xfff, or 0xc30 when the viewport spills outside the window region), four clip offsets and the window-relative origin, followed by the viewport engine handler; when the flag at offset 0x78d is set it also sends a second handler through port 0x43c. **Inferred:** the offsets and limit shift the guard region around the window; only the arithmetic is read from code. |
| `resolve_scissor` | 0x0c018a00 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x18a00). Clamps the scissor box (state-block offsets 0x844-0x850, used only when the scissor flag at 0x76c is set) to the window rectangle and the framebuffer bounds, stores the four resulting limits at offsets 0x87a-0x881, writes the horizontal extent to engine memory 0xc04, programs raster registers 0x01133fc2-0x01143fc3 with the packed x and y ranges through descriptor 0x58404800, and ends by calling `resolve_viewport`. |
| `arm_scissor` | 0x0c018c90 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x18c90). Token 0x00c handler (scissor call): reads x, y, width and height, rejects a negative width or height as an invalid value, stores the four values at state-block offsets 0x844-0x850 and jumps to `resolve_scissor`. |
| `scissor_pop` | 0x0c019b5c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x19b5c). One branch to the scissor resolver, which recomputes the hardware scissor rectangle from the state block; it is the scissor step of the sequence that re-applies state after a pop of the saved attributes, and it has no code of its own. |
| `PolygonStippleGLRotate` | 0x0c026b24 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x26b24). Computes how far the stored polygon stipple pattern must be rotated. Adds two pairs of sixteen-bit state values (bytes 0x86c-0x885), keeps each sum modulo 32, compares them with the previous rotation kept in the context record at offsets 0x10c0-0x10c4, and stores the new values. It sends the x and y rotation changes to the operand and data ports, starts the engine handler addressed by the low half of the word at 0xc8, and, when the shared pattern memory currently holds the polygon stipple, sends a second engine handler from table offset 0x35c. **Inferred:** the two pairs are the stipple anchor and the window origin, from their use in the stipple finish and clip validation. |
| `arm_polygonstipple` | 0x0c02c7bc | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2c7bc). Token handler 0x00b. Sets a fixed 32 by 32 one-component image description in the pixel state, reads the pointer flag and data-present words, clears the transfer flags, writes distribution mode 0 through descriptor 0x44000020, sets the operation word to 0x101, parses the host image, runs the index-stream splitter, the polygon-stipple engine stage and the distributor set-up, streams the pixels, then runs the stipple end-of-stream routine or stores it as a continuation. |
| `arm_rasterpos4d` | 0x0c0360fc | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x360fc). Token 0xb0 handler. Reads four double-precision coordinates (eight words) from the host-interface block, loads the engine transfer mask from the state-block word at offset 0xc94, sends the eight words to the engines under command descriptor 0xf869e800, then restores the mask from offset 0xc90 and returns. Sets the current raster position. |
| `arm_rasterpos4f` | 0x0c0361b4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x361b4). Token 0xb1 handler. Reads four single-precision coordinates from the host-interface block, loads the engine transfer mask from the state-block word at offset 0xc94, sends the four words to the engines under command descriptor 0xd869e800, then restores the mask from offset 0xc90 by tail-calling the mask setter. |
| `arm_rasterpos4i` | 0x0c036230 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x36230). Token 0xb2 handler. Same sequence as the single-precision form but for four 32-bit integer coordinates: reads four words, sets the engine transfer mask from the state-block word at offset 0xc94, sends them under command descriptor 0xd829e800 and restores the mask from offset 0xc90. |
| `arm_rasterpos4s` | 0x0c0362ac | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x362ac). Token 0xb3 handler. Same sequence for four 16-bit integer coordinates (one word each): sets the engine transfer mask from offset 0xc94, sends the words under command descriptor 0xa829e800, restores the mask from offset 0xc90. Additionally writes 1 to distributor register 0x06400020 first and, when bit 0x40 of the state-block byte at offset 0xcf0 is set, writes 0 to it afterwards. |
| `arm_set_current_raster` | 0x0c036350 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x36350). Token 0x10e handler. Selects all engines through port 0x438, forwards fifteen payload words (fourteen from the host-interface block plus the last one) to the engines through port 0x840, then writes the engine handler address that the table gives for `mgeb_SetCurrentRaster` to port 0x8c0. Sets the current raster state in every engine; touches no state-block word. |
| `rasterpos_init` | 0x0c036388 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x36388). Resets the default raster position: sets the engine transfer mask from the state-block word at offset 0xc94, sends three words of -1.0 under command descriptor 0xda69e800, restores the mask from offset 0xc90, then writes one zero word to engine memory at address 0x23. Called once, from the late state initialiser; it is not a token handler. |
| `tbus_write_word` | 0x0c036408 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x36408). Writes one raster-bus word: sends descriptor 0x5847c800 to the descriptor port, then the first and third arguments to the operand port 0x840 and the second argument to the final port 0x8c0, and returns. The shared writer used by the texture, pixel and accumulation set-up code (28 call sites). |
| `InitMSPointsGlobal` | 0x0c040008 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x40008). Loads the packed multisample point data `MSPoints_Infos` into engine memory: for two sets of seven tables it sends 0x640 words in chunks of 256 through operand port 0x478 (the engine address, from 0x1c07 up) and data port 0x840, each chunk started by the handler in the packed table at state-block offset 0x9c. |
| `initStipplePat` | 0x0404d538 | **Established** (`arm1.u` 6.5.30m). 32 words of all ones: the default polygon stipple that `InitPolygonStippleGEState` loads so an unstippled polygon draws every pixel. |
| `MSPoints_Infos` | 0x04051e0c | **Established** (`arm1.u` 6.5.30m). 0x15e00 bytes of packed sample-coverage data that `InitMSPointsGlobal` expands and loads into engine memory for drawing multisampled points. |

## Texture management

Texture binding, loading, the ARM texture cache and texture-format tables.

| Symbol | Address | What it is and does |
|---|---|---|
| `arm_texgend` | 0x0c024d50 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x24d50). Token handler 0x013: loads the address of its message text and tail-calls the console printer, which prints that host-side texture-generation-double entry is not implemented and asks for the float form. It reads no payload, so the payload words of the command are then parsed as the next command, and it sends nothing to the engines. |
| `arm_texgeni` | 0x0c024d94 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x24d94). Token handler 0x015: reuses the not-implemented message of the double-precision handler, tail-calls the console printer with it and returns without reading any payload or touching the engines. |
| `arm_texgenf` | 0x0c024d9c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x24d9c). Token handler 0x014. Reads nine payload words: the coordinate (0x2000-0x2003), the parameter name, and seven value words. Any other coordinate, or a name outside the generation-mode, plane and distance-plane set, raises an invalid-enum error and the command ends; a mode-name value is also checked and kept in the state block at offset 0x804-0x810, one word per coordinate. Valid commands send the coordinate to port 0x478, the name and all seven values to port 0x840 and the generation engine handler (high half of state-block word 0xec) to port 0x8c0. |
| `e_texgen_s` | 0x0c024ecc | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x24ecc). Enable routine for texture coordinate S generation (GL enable 0xc60), reached through the enable dispatch table and from the pop routines: sets the flag byte at state-block offset 0x771, sends 0xc60 to port 0x478, loads the enable handler (high half of state-block word 0xf0) into port 0x8c0, then tail-jumps to the transfer-mask routine to re-derive which attributes the engines transfer. |
| `d_texgen_s` | 0x0c024eec | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x24eec). Disable routine for texture coordinate S generation (GL enable 0xc60), reached through the disable dispatch table and from the pop routines: clears the flag byte at state-block offset 0x771, sends 0xc60 to port 0x478, loads the disable handler (low half of state-block word 0xec) into port 0x8c0, then tail-jumps to the transfer-mask routine. |
| `e_texgen_t` | 0x0c024f10 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x24f10). Enables texture coordinate T generation (0xc61): sets the flag byte at state-block offset 0x772, sends 0xc61 to port 0x478 and the enable handler (high half of state-block word 0xf0) to port 0x8c0, then tail-jumps to the transfer-mask routine as the S form does. |
| `d_texgen_t` | 0x0c024f34 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x24f34). Disables texture coordinate T generation (0xc61): clears the flag byte at state-block offset 0x772, sends 0xc61 to port 0x478 and the disable handler (low half of state-block word 0xec) to port 0x8c0, then tail-jumps to the transfer-mask routine. |
| `e_texgen_r` | 0x0c024f5c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x24f5c). Enables texture coordinate R generation (0xc62): sets the flag byte at state-block offset 0x773, sends 0xc62 to port 0x478 and the enable handler (high half of state-block word 0xf0) to port 0x8c0, then returns. Unlike the S and T forms it does not re-derive the transfer mask. |
| `d_texgen_r` | 0x0c024f80 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x24f80). Disables texture coordinate R generation (0xc62): clears the flag byte at state-block offset 0x773, sends 0xc62 to port 0x478 and the disable handler (low half of state-block word 0xec) to port 0x8c0, then returns without re-deriving the transfer mask. |
| `e_texgen_q` | 0x0c024fa8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x24fa8). Enables texture coordinate Q generation (0xc63): sets the flag byte at state-block offset 0x774, sends 0xc63 to port 0x478 and the enable handler (high half of state-block word 0xf0) to port 0x8c0, then returns without re-deriving the transfer mask. |
| `d_texgen_q` | 0x0c024fcc | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x24fcc). Disables texture coordinate Q generation (0xc63): clears the flag byte at state-block offset 0x774, sends 0xc63 to port 0x478 and the disable handler (low half of state-block word 0xec) to port 0x8c0, then returns without re-deriving the transfer mask. |
| `arm_texenvf` | 0x0c024ff4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x24ff4). Token handler 0x011 for a float texture-environment parameter. Reads five payload words: the parameter name goes to port 0x47c, the next four values go to port 0x840 in turn, and the texture-environment engine handler (low half of state-block word 0x120) goes to port 0x8c0. It keeps no copy of the values. |
| `arm_texenvi` | 0x0c025030 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x25030). Token handler 0x012 for an integer texture-environment parameter. Reads four payload words and the final word. The first word, the parameter name, is stored to port 0x8c0 at once and port 0x428 is cleared. For name 0x2200 it loads descriptor 0x58001000, sends the second word to port 0x8c0 and returns after writing the low half of state-block word 0x120 to port 0x4fc. For other names it loads descriptor 0x59001000, sends the next three words to port 0x840 and the final word to port 0x8c0, then writes the same word to port 0x4fc. |
| `arm_texload_setup` | 0x0c025134 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x25134). Token handler 0x175: a single return instruction, so the command does nothing and consumes no payload. |
| `flush_texload_ids` | 0x0c025138 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x25138). Writes -1 into the first word of each of the 513 eight-byte entries of `tex_cache`, marking every cached load id invalid. Called when the 20-bit load generation counter wraps and when a context is activated. |
| `update_texload_id` | 0x0c025168 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x25168). Advances the 20-bit texture-load generation counter at state-block offset 0x8b8. On wrap to zero it flushes every cached load id, sends the new value as raster-bus operand 0x1002 and loads the fill handler through port 0x4f4; otherwise it stores the new value in the first word of the cache entry whose index is the first argument. Always ends by sending the counter as operand 0x5003 through port 0x474. Called by the load-id token handler and by the texture swap-out path. |
| `arm_texload_id` | 0x0c0251fc | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x251fc). Token handler 0x176: reads one payload word (the cache index) and tail-jumps to the routine that advances the texture-load generation counter and stores it in that cache entry. |
| `arm_active_texture` | 0x0c025204 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x25204). Token handler 0x177: reads the active texture target (0-3) into state-block offset 0x890, sends 0 to port 0x47c when it is zero and -1 otherwise, loads the texture engine handler (high half of state-block word 0x110) into port 0x8c0, then tail-jumps to the transfer-mask routine. |
| `flush_texbind_ids` | 0x0c02522c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2522c). Writes -1 into the second word of each of the 513 eight-byte entries of `tex_cache`, marking every cached bind id invalid. Called when the 20-bit bind generation counter wraps and when a context is activated. |
| `update_texbind_id` | 0x0c025258 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x25258). Advances the 20-bit texture-bind generation counter at state-block offset 0x8bc. On wrap to zero it flushes every cached bind id, sends the new value as raster-bus operand 0x5002 and loads the fill handler through port 0x4f4; otherwise it stores the new value in the second word of the cache entry whose index is the first argument. Always ends by sending the counter as operand 0x1003. Called by the bind-id token handler and by the texture swap-in path. |
| `arm_texbind_id` | 0x0c0252ec | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x252ec). Token handler 0x178: reads one payload word (the cache index) and tail-jumps to the routine that advances the texture-bind generation counter and stores it in that cache entry. |
| `arm_bind_arm_tex_cache` | 0x0c0252f4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x252f4). Token handler 0x1c4: reads one payload word, an index into `tex_cache`, and writes -1 into both words of that eight-byte entry, so its load id and bind id read as invalid. It sends nothing to the hardware. |
| `arm_free_arm_tex_cache` | 0x0c025310 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x25310). Token handler 0x1c5: reads a cache index. When the entry's load id is valid it writes that id, the fill-handler selector and the current load counter to the raster-bus register writer; when its bind id is valid it does the same for the bind id and bind counter. It leaves the cache entry itself unchanged, so the ids still read as valid afterwards. |
| `sendTexTable` | 0x0c02539c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2539c). Copies one texture record to the raster boards with the bus word writer. The first argument is the table index (records of 33 words from state-block pointer 0xcf4 plus 0x86c); the second is the kind. Kind 0 writes 33 words to bus addresses 0x1820-0x1840; other kinds write 16 word pairs starting at 0x1810 (kind 1) or 0x1800, the second word of each pair merged into the address. |
| `get_addr` | 0x0c025438 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x25438). Payload reader shared by the three-dimensional bind handlers. The first argument is a destination pointer and the second a word count: it reads count minus one words from the host-interface block into consecutive destination words, then stores the final-read word after them. With a null destination it discards those words and returns the final word. |
| `set_bicubic_or_3d_addr` | 0x0c025490 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x25490). Writes one raster-bus word for a texture address pair given by pointer: bus address 0x10040000 ORed with bits 8-23 of the second word, data the first word with the second word's low byte placed in bits 24-31. Tail-calls the bus word writer. Used by the bicubic and three-dimensional bind handlers and when a context is activated. |
| `set_3d_mipmap_addr` | 0x0c0254bc | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x254bc). Writes up to six address pairs of a three-dimensional mipmap texture from the block at the argument, each pair to two consecutive raster-bus addresses (0x100c0000-0x10150000), using the same address and data packing as the bicubic form. It stops before a pair when a word tested so far has bit 31 set, which marks an unused entry. |
| `arm_change_post_tex_scale_bias` | 0x0c025688 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x25688). Token handler 0x1d7: reads a texture id and three value words, returns when the id is not the bound texture target at state-block offset 0x890, otherwise loads descriptor 0x5850d800 and sends 1 and two of the words to port 0x840 and the last to port 0x8c0. |
| `arm_bind_texture` | 0x0c0256c8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x256c8). Token handler 0x1d5, the general texture bind. Reads a cache index and a flags word (target in bits 28-31, format in bits 12-23, mode in bits 24-27). Releases the entry's old load id when the index is valid; adopts a higher target (state-block offset 0x890) only when its enable flag is set, otherwise discards the remaining words and returns; re-sends the component count and re-validates the colour table on a format change; sends the table for the mode (descriptor 0x58404800, raster register 0x014e1019 for mode 1); then the target and two words under the texture engine handler. |
| `arm_bind_linear_texture` | 0x0c0259ac | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x259ac). Token handler 0x106, the bind for a linear texture. Like the general bind it releases the entry's old load id, adopts a higher target only when its enable flag is set (else discards two words and returns) and re-sends the component count on a format change, but it stores the next word at state-block offset 0x894 and mode 3 at 0x898 and sends no table. It ends with the target at port 0x47c and three words at port 0x840 under the texture engine handler. |
| `arm_bind_2d_clipmap_texture` | 0x0c025b8c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x25b8c). Token handler 0x1aa: reads eight words. Bit 22 of the fifth word selects a five-register clipmap raster write (operand 5 at port 0x474, first register 0x01501020) or a two-register one (operand 2). It then sends the clipmap transform handler's operands, either -1 at port 0x478 with the sixth to eighth words, or zero with 1.0 float and zeros when the sixth word is zero, and finally loads the clipmap transform handler. |
| `arm_bind_3d_mipmap_texture` | 0x0c025ccc | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x25ccc). Token handler 0x17b: reads four words. If the current target is not three-dimensional it needs the enable flag at state-block offset 0x770 (else it discards ten more words and returns) and sets the target to 3; then reads ten words into offsets 0x8c0-0x8e4, writes five raster registers (the first 0x01471000) from the four words with operand 5 at port 0x474, and tail-calls the routine that writes the mipmap address pairs. |
| `arm_bind_bicubic_texture` | 0x0c025da8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x25da8). Token handler 0x17a: reads three words. If the current target differs it adopts one- or two-dimensional only when that texture's enable flag is set, else discards two words and returns. Otherwise it reads two words into state-block offset 0x8c0, writes the bicubic address, then raster registers 0x01471000 and 0x014f13d5 with operand 2 through port 0x840. |
| `arm_bind_3d_texture` | 0x0c025e6c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x25e6c). Token handler 0x17c: reads two words. If the current target is not three-dimensional it needs the enable flag at state-block offset 0x770 (else discards two words and returns) and sets the target to 3; then reads two words into state-block offset 0x8c0, writes raster registers 0x01471000 and 0x014f13d5 with operand 2 and writes the address pair through the bicubic address writer. |
| `arm_bind_detail_texture` | 0x0c025efc | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x25efc). Token handler 0x17d for a detail texture: releases the cache entry's old load id when valid, writes 0xff and register 0x014d3fd6 with a value built from two payload words through descriptor 0x58404800, stores three more words at state-block offsets 0x8a4-0x8ac, optionally writes register 0x014e1019 when the base texture matches, and ends by writing register 0x01493fd4 with the texture flags word from offset 0x894. |
| `arm_tex_send_arm_table` | 0x0c026010 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x26010). Token handler 0x17e: reads a table index, then 33 payload words into the texture record at that index (33 words each, from the pointer at state-block offset 0xcf4 plus 0x86c). Nothing is sent to the hardware; the table is later written out by the table-sending routine. |
| `arm_tex_arm_cache_index_invalid` | 0x0c02604c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2604c). Token handler 0x1e0: reads a kind (0-2) and an index; if the remembered texture index of that kind (one word per kind in the state block between offsets 0x8a0 and 0x8b4) equals the index, sets it to -1. Nothing is sent to the hardware. |
| `initTextureState` | 0x0c02609c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2609c). Called by the context initialiser to put texture defaults into the state block and engines: sets the remembered indexes at offsets 0x8a0-0x8b4 to -1, zeroes both generation counters, writes six texture-environment function tables of five words into engine external memory, then sends three defaults to the texture-environment handler: parameter 0x2200 with value 0x46040000 (float 8448.0, the modulate mode), 0x2201 with four zeros, and 0x80be with four zeros. Also sets the four generation modes at offsets 0x804-0x810 to 0x2400. |
| `arm_shadow_texture` | 0x0c026218 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x26218). Token handler 0x1c7: reads two words and the final word, then writes three raster-register pairs through port 0x840 with operand 3 at port 0x474: 0x0159101f gets the first word, 0x015a0c02 gets the second doubled and 0x0158101e gets the final word. Ends by loading the bus register write handler. |
| `activate_texture` | 0x0c026288 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x26288). Called by the context activation routine to restore texture hardware state: re-validates the colour table, rewrites the address words for the current target kind (the bicubic or the mipmap writer), zeroes both generation counters, resends the table of the remembered kind, writes the counters to the raster-bus register writer and flushes every cached load id and bind id. |
| `texture_pop` | 0x0c0263bc | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x263bc). Called by the attribute pop: re-issues each of the one-, two- and three-dimensional texture enables from the flag bytes at state-block offsets 0x76e-0x770, sends the component count, re-issues the four generation enables from offsets 0x771-0x774, then sends each coordinate's saved mode (state-block words 0x804-0x810, parameter name 0x2500, three zero values) to the generation engine handler. |
| `arm_texgen_pop` | 0x0c026554 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x26554). Token handler 0x1d6: reads one word from the second payload port and discards it, sends 0x3e3 to port 0x478, writes 0x04000070 to the control register at 0x200058 so the next 0x70 host words go straight to the distributor, waits for the distributor, and loads the engine-memory load handler, so those words load engine memory. |
| `arm_texparameterf` | 0x0c026594 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x26594). Token handler 0x00d: a single branch to the bad-command routine, which prints the token numbers, reads and prints 100 payload words and then spins; the firmware implements no real handler for this token. |
| `arm_texparameteri` | 0x0c026598 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x26598). Token handler 0x00e: a single branch to the bad-command routine, which prints the token numbers, reads and prints 100 payload words and then spins; the firmware implements no real handler for this token. |
| `arm_teximage1d` | 0x0c02659c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2659c). Token handler 0x00f: a single branch to the bad-command routine, which prints the token numbers, reads and prints 100 payload words and then spins; the firmware implements no real handler for this token. |
| `arm_teximage2d` | 0x0c0265a0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x265a0). Token handler 0x010: a single branch to the bad-command routine, which prints the token numbers, reads and prints 100 payload words and then spins; the firmware implements no real handler for this token. |
| `arm_copyteximage1d` | 0x0c0265f4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x265f4). Token handler 0x0fc: a single branch to the bad-command routine, which prints the token numbers, reads and prints 100 payload words and then spins; the firmware implements no real handler for this token. |
| `arm_copytexsubimage1d` | 0x0c0265f8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x265f8). Token handler 0x0fe: a single branch to the bad-command routine, which prints the token numbers, reads and prints 100 payload words and then spins; the firmware implements no real handler for this token. |
| `arm_copytexsubimage2d` | 0x0c026604 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x26604). Token handler 0x101: a single branch to the bad-command routine, which prints the token numbers, reads and prints 100 payload words and then spins; the firmware implements no real handler for this token. |
| `TexReadWar` | 0x0c029428 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x29428). Texture read-back loop used when the top nibble of the board-capability word at data offset 0x120 is clear. For each row group (the count at 0x904) it cuts the row into pieces of three times the component size from the word at 0x9d4, and for each piece programs the raster-bus register and the texture-read address, size and stride registers (0x450-0x456), starts the texture read and the raster bus, then writes tbus register 0x1415 and zero to 0x477. Called by `__glkonSendPixelsToHost`. |
| `HugeTexReadLM` | 0x0c029638 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x29638). Texture read-back for line-mode transfers of a texture larger than one 4096-line window. Walks the rows in chunks of at most 4096 lines, and for each chunk programs the raster-bus register, starts the texture read and the raster bus, writes 1 to tbus register 0x1415, then pads with zero writes to register 0x455, as many as the engine count minus a per-pass value at 0xc38. |
| `HugeTexRead` | 0x0c029770 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x29770). Texture read-back for textures larger than 4096 in either direction. Loops over blocks of at most 4096 rows and, inside each, blocks of at most 4096 columns. For each block it writes the size registers 0x455-0x456, programs the raster-bus register, writes the texture-read registers 0x450-0x454, starts the read with `StartTexRead` and the raster bus, then writes 1 to register 0x1415 and zero to 0x477. |
| `arm_texelstorei` | 0x0c02af20 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2af20). Token handler 0x1bb. Marks the pixel state dirty, reads a parameter name and value, and stores the texel-store parameters in the pixel shadow at 0x260+0xc4-0x100: the names in the 0xcf0 and 0xd00 groups at fixed slots (two as single bytes), and the extension names 0x806b-0x806e. The two alignment-style names are kept only when the value is a power of two up to eight. It writes no hardware. |
| `log2` | 0x0c02b690 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2b690). Counts how many times the argument can be shifted right before it reaches zero and returns that count, so it returns the position of the highest set bit plus one (0 for 0), not the floor of a base-2 logarithm. Used by the texture swap routines on a power-of-two size, where the result is the exponent plus one. Leaf routine, no state. |
| `arm_drawtexels` | 0x0c02b7a8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2b7a8). Token handler 0x174. Reads the texture target and about twenty image words (size, format, type, offsets, flags) from the host-interface block into the pixel state, takes the final payload word as a pointer flag, clears bit 0x20 of the transfer flags, sets the per-target unit (0 for target 0xde0, the context value for 0xde1, else 1) in two places, calls `drawtexels` with argument 0, then restores bits 0x10400 of the saved flags. |
| `drawtexels` | 0x0c02b8cc | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2b8cc). Common body of the texture download used by the token handler and the swap-in routine. Writes distribution mode 0 through descriptor 0x44000020, sets the operation word to 0x81, programs the drawing buffers, re-resolves the pixel path if the state is dirty, parses the host image, sets up the sub-texture load at position 0, calls the raster set-up, split, distributor and engine-stage slots of the chosen path, applies the texture size workaround for five of the paths, and streams the data. It then runs the finish slot, or stores it as a continuation. |
| `arm_copyteximage` | 0x0c02bf60 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2bf60). Token handler 0x0fd. Reads the source rectangle, target, level, format and offset words into the pixel state, takes the final word as the data flag, and tail-calls the video-source form when the video flag at +0x348 is set. Otherwise it writes distribution mode 0 through descriptor 0x44000020, sets drawing buffers, resolves the copy path, sets the raster read-back and a sub-texture load at position 0, runs the engine slots, applies the texture size workaround for five of the paths, starts the raster bus and jumps to the path's finish slot. |
| `arm_getteximage` | 0x0c02c298 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2c298). Token handler 0x052. Reads the target, level, format, type, rectangle and pack words from the host-interface block into the pixel state and the final word, sets the unit field (0 for target 0xde0, the context value for 0xde1, else 1) in two places, and tail-calls `getteximage` with argument 0. |
| `getteximage` | 0x0c02c358 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2c358). Common body of the texture read-back, used by the token handler and the swap-out routine. Writes distribution mode 0 through descriptor 0x44000020, sets the operation word to 0x24, marks the state dirty, parses the host image, resolves the read path, sets up the texture read, runs the path's split slot, the packer set-up and the engine slots, sends the pixels to the host and jumps to the path's finish slot. The argument is passed on to the send routine. |
| `SetTextureWorkaround` | 0x0c02cb94 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2cb94). Rewrites three fields of the output descriptor block at pixel state +0xd4 for a texture transfer. With total = width times height, unit = three times the block's size word, q = total / unit and n = the word at +0x54 of the board-configuration block, it stores ((q mod n) + 1) mod n at +0xc, the rounded-up (q + 1) / n at +0x14 and total minus unit times q at +0x10. Called before the engine slots of texture downloads and copies on five of the paths. **Inferred:** a hardware work-around, from the name only; the code shows just these divisions. |
| `ResolveTextureInternalFormat` | 0x0c036424 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x36424). Classifies the texture's internal format, the third word of the record given as the first argument, into the texture-state word at offset 4. One pair of adjacent format codes starting at 0x8047 and a second pair starting at 0x8058 give class 1, the top pair (starting at 0x805a) gives class 2, every other format in the span 0x803b-0x805b gives class 0, and a code outside the span leaves the word unchanged. Called by `arm_texsetup`. |
| `arm_texsetup` | 0x0c0364e0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x364e0). Token 0x173 handler. Reads the texture-load payload: two words (the image width and height) into state-block offsets 0x900-0x904, then the rest into a stack record and the texture-state block, forces the target word to 0x0de1 (two-dimensional texture) and calls the format classifier and then the raster-register writer for texture loads. Nothing is sent to the engines directly. |
| `PixRM_TextureLoadSetup` | 0x0c036570 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x36570). Writes the raster-register set that prepares a texture load, all through descriptor 0x58404800: the format-class and flag word to register 0x01407fc5, the offsets to the next register, the base-two logarithms of width, height and (target 0x806f only) depth to a third, then the extent minus one and the base address to two more, and finally one raster-bus word with the shared word writer. Called by `arm_texsetup`. |
| `SetupRM_TextureBind` | 0x0c0367c8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x367c8). Writes the raster registers that bind a texture, through descriptor 0x58404800: size exponents to register 0x01483fd5, the base to the next, the filter, wrap and format bits (format code looked up in a conversion table by internal format) to a third, two parameter registers, a depth parameter register only for target 0x806f, then one raster-bus word.
