Reads the texture-state block and the sizes at state-block offsets 0x900-0x904. Called by `arm_texbind`. |
| `GetTexManageBindParam` | 0x0c036a10 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x36a10). Reads the texture-bind payload (36 words) from the host-interface block: two into state-block offsets 0x900-0x904, one into the caller's record, seventeen into fixed words of the texture-state block, then sixteen consecutive words from offset 0x34, the last from the checksum position. Sends nothing to hardware. Called by `arm_texbind`. |
| `arm_texbind` | 0x0c036ae4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x36ae4). Token 0x16 handler. Sets the texture-state word at offset 0x98 to 1, reads the bind payload into a 36-byte stack record, writes the bind registers from it, and clears the word again. Calls `GetTexManageBindParam` and then `SetupRM_TextureBind`; no engine handler is sent. |
| `texFormatConvert` | 0x0404db08 | **Established** (`arm1.u` 6.5.30m). 33 words translating a texture's internal format to the raster boards' texel format number (up to 0xe); `SetupRM_TextureBind` reads it when binding a texture. |
| `nComponents` | 0x040516f0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` 6.5.30m). Six words of components per texture colour-table format (one to four) that `PixValidateTexColorTable` uses to size the table load. |
| `texFormatLookup` | 0x04051708 | **Established** (`arm1.u` 6.5.30m). 63 words mapping a texture internal format and colour-table format pair to the resulting texel layout (values 1-5); read by `PixValidateTexColorTable`. |
| `tex_cache` | 0x0409086c | **Established** (`arm1.u` 6.5.30m). The ARM's texture cache table of id pairs, invalidated by writing -1 to both words; the bind handlers, `flush_texload_ids` and `update_texbind_id` keep it. |
| `texState` | 0x04095334 | **Established** (`arm1.u` 6.5.30m). 0xa4 bytes of current texture set-up shared by `arm_texsetup`, `arm_texbind` and the raster-board load and bind routines, including the resolved internal format. |

## Pixel path: set-up and state

How a pixel command is classified and its state resolved before the hardware is programmed.

| Symbol | Address | What it is and does |
|---|---|---|
| `arm_validate_clip` | 0x0c018168 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x18168). Token 0x1f3 handler (window clip validation): refuses with a printed privilege message unless the privileged status bit is set, reads eleven payload words (window and clip rectangles and the window id) into halfword fields of the state block at offsets 0x864-0x88f, programs three raster registers through two raster descriptors, calls `resolve_scissor`, sends the packed window origin word (operand 0x1c3c at port 0x474, data at port 0x840), and ends in `PolygonStippleGLRotate`. |
| `arm_validate_readclip` | 0x0c018574 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x18574). Token 0x1ee handler (read-buffer clip validation): refuses with a printed privilege message unless the privileged status bit is set, reads two payload words and the final word, stores them as halfword fields at state-block offsets 0x886-0x88b and adds the change in the final word to fourteen state bytes at offsets 0x1ea-0x1ef and 0x1f6-0x1fd. It sends nothing to the engines or ports. |
| `arm_pixeltransferi` | 0x0c0265a8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x265a8). Token handler 0x031: a single branch to the bad-command routine, which prints the token numbers, reads and prints 100 payload words and then spins; the firmware implements no real handler for this token. |
| `arm_pixelstoref` | 0x0c0265ac | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x265ac). Token handler 0x032: a single branch to the bad-command routine, which prints the token numbers, reads and prints 100 payload words and then spins; the firmware implements no real handler for this token. |
| `arm_copycolortable` | 0x0c0265f0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x265f0). Token handler 0x0e9: a single branch to the bad-command routine, which prints the token numbers, reads and prints 100 payload words and then spins; the firmware implements no real handler for this token. |
| `SetDB` | 0x0c026874 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x26874). Change tracker for the pixel path. Compares the pixel flag word at 0xc88, the transfer-state word at 0x908, the width and height words at 0x900-0x904 and the three arguments with copies kept at 0xc70-0xc80. For each value that differs it stores the new copy and sets one bit of the dirty word at 0x90c (one of the bits 0x1-0x40). The first call after reset (marker byte at 0xc7f clear) sets bit 0x80 and fills every copy. Called before a draw, copy or texture-copy transfer so later stages reload only what changed. |
| `InitPixState` | 0x0c026cb8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x26cb8). Per-context pixel defaults, called from context creation. Calls in turn the distributor pixel defaults, the framebuffer state, the fast-state, pixel-GL-state, slow-state and input-state initialisers, the polygon-stipple, colour-table and histogram initialisers and the divide-modulo table initialiser, then tail-jumps to `BuildStreamWramModel`. |
| `InitFastState` | 0x0c026e68 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x26e68). Resets the change tracker used by the pixel path: zeroes the cached copies at pixel-block offsets 0x370-0x380, the first-call marker byte at 0x37f and the flag word at 0xc88, and sets the word after it (0xc8c) to 8. The next transfer then reloads every stage. |
| `InitInputState` | 0x0c026eac | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x26eac). Pixel-store and overlap defaults: stores 0x7fffffff at pixel-block offset 0x9e8, zero at the overlap-enable word 0xc48 and the dirty word 0x90c, and points the two pixel-store record pointers at 0x9b4-0x9b8 to the records at state-block offsets 0x2dc-0x300. |
| `InitSlowState` | 0x0c026edc | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x26edc). Defaults for the rarely changed pixel state in the context record (the block at pointer 0xcf4, offsets 0x564-0x6cc). Fills three records, one each for the one-dimensional, two-dimensional and separable convolution filters, with a promoted size looked up per kind, format 0x1908, type 0x1401, scale 1.0 and zero bias. It also stores 0x8421 at 0x6b8 and 0xffff at 0x6cc, clears the pending-path words and loads each default kernel through the kernel loader. |
| `InitGeTypeFormatTable` | 0x0c02710c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2710c). Builds, once at start-up, the engine type-and-format table: 15 host data types by 21 pixel formats, one 32-byte record each. Each record holds a shift and its power of two taken from a per-format table, a second shift and its power of two from a per-type table, a per-type size word, a per-format word copied twice, and the product of the size and format words. Called from the pixel-globals initialiser. |
| `InitPixelGLState` | 0x0c0271c4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x271c4). Defaults for the pixel transfer and pixel store state at state-block offsets 0x260-0x36c. Sets the colour scale factors to 1.0 and the biases, flag bytes and counters to zero, sets both pixel-store alignments to 4, stores 0x81c5 in the transform-mode word at 0x36c, then tail-jumps to `e_pixel_transform` to turn the pixel transform on. |
| `SetFbState` | 0x0c0272e8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x272e8). Maps a buffer configuration code in the first argument to a small framebuffer format number and stores it in the draw-format and read-format words at context-record offsets 0x650-0x654. Code 1 gives format 1, code 9 gives 2, code 2 gives 3, codes three, eight, ten, eleven and twelve give 4, and code 0 gives 4 only when state byte 0x1cc is 1, else 0. Any other code leaves both words unchanged. |
| `SetFbReadState` | 0x0c027360 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x27360). Maps the read-buffer configuration state to the read-format word at context-record offset 0x654. The read-buffer byte at state offset 0x1fe selects: value 5 gives format 1, value 2 gives 3, value 3 gives 2 when byte 0x1e2 is zero and 4 otherwise, and value 0 gives 4 only when byte 0x1e0 is 1, else 0. Any other value leaves the word unchanged. Called by the read-buffer configuration handler. |
| `InitFbState` | 0x0c0273cc | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x273cc). Framebuffer defaults for a new context. Selects framebuffer format 1 through the draw-format setter, clears the readback flag at 0xca4, then writes raster register 0x0132080d twice through descriptor 0x58404800, first value 0x0f with select 0x0f and then 0xf0 with select 0xf0. |
| `ParseHostImage` | 0x0c027420 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x27420). Turns the host-side image description in the pixel block into a byte layout. Bit 0x1 of the word at 0x908 selects the source or destination description and its pixel-store record. It stores the element count and size for the data type and format (a one-bit mask form for the bitmap type), the row stride rounded by the alignment table, the row padding, the total size, and the starting byte offset including skipped rows, pixels and bits. Called before every draw, read, bitmap, stipple, filter and colour-table transfer. |
| `QueryOverlap` | 0x0c0275a4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x275a4). Starts the check whether a copy's source and destination overlap. With the overlap-enable word 0xc48 set it forces the flag to zero. Otherwise it sets the flag when the source buffer bit, chosen from the exclusive-or of bytes 0x1f4 and 0x1f0, appears in the buffer mask byte 0x1f5, whose bit order is swapped when byte 0x1dc is set. When the flag is set it writes 1 to the geometry-engine mailbox valid register (host-interface block minus 0x164), sends the first argument to port 0x474, the other arguments to the data port and an engine handler address from table offset 0x354 to port 0x8c0. |
| `WaitOverlap` | 0x0c027664 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x27664). Collects the answer to the overlap query. If the query flag is set it polls bit 0 of the geometry-engine mailbox valid register (host-interface block minus 0x164), reads the mailbox data word and writes 1 back to the valid register. It then folds the data into a result mask: bit 0 when the 0xc field is nonzero and the low two bits are 1, bit 1 (value 2) when the low bits are nonzero and the 0xc field is 4, and value 4 when bit 0x10 is set. With the flag clear it returns zero. |
| `__glkonFbToRbusFmt` | 0x0c0276e0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x276e0). Returns the raster-bus format code for the current read format: loads the read-format word at context-record offset 0x654 and indexes a word table with it. |
| `__glkonGetTbusFmt` | 0x0c0276f8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x276f8). Returns the texture-bus format code for a host data type (first argument) and pixel format (second argument): a word read from a table of 21 entries per type. |
| `commonResolve` | 0x0c027714 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x27714). Classifies a pixel transfer and fills a six-word result record. Depending on the feature word on the stack it takes the source from a host image, the framebuffer or a texture, and looks up the raster-bus format codes and format signatures for source and destination in the format tables. It works out which colour channels (lane bits 0x1-0x8) pass through unchanged, a mode word with the 0x10 and 0x20 bits, a single-channel flag and a pass-through flag. It also sets or clears bit 0x400 and bit 0x10000 of the flag word in its fourth argument. Called by every path-resolve routine. |
| `arm_lazymode` | 0x0c027e14 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x27e14). Token handler 0x1ce (HP_LAZYMODE). Reads one payload word. When it is zero the handler sets bit 0x200 of the flag word at pixel-block offset 0x90c, otherwise it clears that bit. The finish routines clear only bits 0-8 of that word, so this bit stays until the next lazy-mode token. |
| `resolveColorPath` | 0x0c027e34 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x27e34). Chooses the pixel path number for a colour transfer and returns it, in the range 0-5 (stream, line-at-a-time or integer short circuit). It decides from the result of the format classifier, the convolution or histogram enable bits (0x3e0 of the flag word at 0xc88) and the source and destination formats, and stores the matching pass-through module number in the fifth word of the result record. It returns the incoming value unchanged when no combination fits. |
| `ResolveDrawPath` | 0x0c0284cc | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x284cc). Chooses the pixel path number for a host-to-framebuffer or texture draw. With the FFT bit (0x80000 of the flag word at 0xc88) set it returns 9 when the framebuffer format is 3 and otherwise prints a message about an illegal format. For colour data it calls the format classifier and then the colour-path chooser, for colour-index data it returns 6, for depth data (bit 0x2000) 7 and for stencil data (bit 0x1000) 8, writing the destination format fields in the pixel block. It returns zero when nothing applies. |
| `ResolveCopyPath` | 0x0c0286cc | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x286cc). Chooses the pixel path number for a framebuffer copy from the copy type word at 0x93c: 0x1800 (colour), 0x1801 (depth) or 0x1802 (stencil). Colour copies return 9 for the FFT case (bit 0x80000 of the flag word at 0xc88), otherwise they run the format classifier, fill the source description from the framebuffer and return the colour-path result or 6 for index data. Depth returns 7 and stencil returns 8, each after filling the source description. Any other type returns zero. |
| `ResolveReadPath` | 0x0c0288f4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x288f4). Chooses the pixel path number for a readback to the host. The source is the framebuffer or texture memory, chosen by bit 0x2 of the word at 0x908. For colour formats it runs the format classifier, fills the source description and returns the colour-path result, or 6 when the format bits are all clear. Depth (bit 0x2000 of the format word at 0x984) returns 7 and stencil (bit 0x1000) returns 8, with a format code looked up in the host-to-bus table. It returns zero when none applies. |
| `resolveVideoPath` | 0x0c028a1c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x28a1c). Path chooser for transfers from a video source. Recomputes the video rectangle fields in the context record at offset 0x6d0 (last column as width plus offset shifted, last row as height minus 1), copies the width and height and the video format defaults into the pixel-block description at 0x914-0x920, runs the format classifier, then tail-calls the colour-path chooser with the result. |
| `setFromTM` | 0x0c028ac8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x28ac8). Fills the source description of a read from texture memory. Copies 32 bytes (eight words) from the `TexTFType` entry for the texture format number at pixel-block offset 0x9bc into the description at 0x914-0x933, and stores the raster-bus packet size from `TexRPacket` for the same format at 0x96c. |
| `__glkonSetFromFB` | 0x0c028b0c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x28b0c). Fills the source description at pixel-block offsets 0x914-0x968 for a read from the framebuffer. Selects by the format number at 0x9bc: numbers 0-6 and 8-11 each store a type code, packet size, mask and unit counts (format 0 uses a second layout when the read format at context offset 0x654 is 2); the others return without changes. |
| `__glkonSetupPacker` | 0x0c028d20 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x28d20). Programs the pixel packer used for readback. Builds one control word from the low two bits of half the word at 0x98c, a flag from the first byte of the record at 0x9b8, a byte-size code looked up per format (6 when bit 0x10 of the word at 0xa00 is set and the path format is 0xa) and a constant 0x40 bit, then writes it to raster register 0x01100441 with select 0x7f through descriptor 0x58404800. |
| `SendPixelsToPipe` | 0x0c028dd8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x28dd8). Starts the host-to-engine pixel stream. Counts the words with the word counter, stores the low half of the word at 0x138 to port 0x4fc, writes 0 to distributor register 0x223 through descriptor 0x44600020, selects descriptor 0x06000020, and (unless the first argument is zero and the word at 0x950 is nonzero) loads the word count, with the first argument as bit 27, into the transfer length register at host-interface block minus 0x1a8. It then marks the context busy, zeroes the progress counters at context offsets 0x708-0x710 and waits for the distributor to finish before clearing the busy word, under the same condition. |
| `NumWordToPipe` | 0x0c028e7c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x28e7c). Returns the number of 32-bit words the host data of the current transfer occupies. With the mode word at 0x954 zero it covers the byte range of size 0x944 starting at offset 0x948, rounded out to whole words. With mode 1 it works out the word counts of four interleaved engine lanes from the row size (bit count for bitmaps, otherwise element size times count), adds them and scales by the row count over 4. Any other mode returns zero. |
| `f2i` | 0x0c0290a0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x290a0). Converts a single-precision float in the first argument to an integer by shifting its 24-bit mantissa right by 23 minus the unbiased exponent. Zero gives zero. The sign bit is dropped, so a negative input returns its magnitude, and values below 1.0 give zero. |
| `arm_pixelzoom` | 0x0c02ad74 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2ad74). Token handler 0x02f. Marks the pixel state dirty (bit 0x80 of the word at pixel state +0xc), reads the two payload words (zoom x and zoom y), stores them at 0x260+0x48 in the shadow, and tail-calls the engine external memory writer to copy those two words to engine external memory address 0xc01 so the engines see the new zoom factors. |
| `arm_pixelstorei` | 0x0c02adb0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2adb0). Token handler 0x033. Marks the pixel state dirty, reads the parameter name and value, and stores the value at the matching offset of the pixel shadow at 0x260+0x7c-0xc0: the pack parameters (swap bytes, LSB first, row length, skip rows, skip pixels, alignment) at 0x7c-0x8c, the unpack parameters at 0xa0-0xb0, and the image-height and skip-images extensions. Names outside these ranges are ignored. It writes no hardware. |
| `arm_pixeltransferf` | 0x0c02b068 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2b068). Token handler 0x030. Marks the pixel state dirty, reads a parameter name and a float, stores it in the pixel shadow at 0x260 (scales and biases for the four colour channels and depth, index shift and offset, post-matrix and post-convolution terms), and updates the transfer-flag word at pixel state +0x388: bit 0 when any scale is not 1.0, bit 1 when any bias is not 0, and bits for map-colour, map-stencil, index shift and index offset. It then writes four summary words at +0x6bc-0x6c8 of the per-context state from the post-colour-matrix scale and bias entries. **Inferred:** those words mark which channels need a non-identity scale and bias stage, from the 1.0 and 0 tests. |
| `do_bitmap` | 0x0c02b39c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2b39c). Body of the bitmap draw for a non-empty bitmap, entered by a branch from `arm_bitmap`. Sets the operation word to 0x41, sends the raster-position words and an engine handler address to the engines (the handler is picked by the mode byte pair at state block +0x862: a table entry, the high half of word +0x11c, or by default the low half of word +0x48), parses the host image, runs the index-stream splitter, the bitmap engine stage and the distributor set-up, writes distribution mode 0 through descriptor 0x44000020, streams the pixels, then either runs the end-of-stream routine or stores its address as a continuation. Restores the saved transfer flags. |
| `arm_bitmap` | 0x0c02b4c0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2b4c0). Token handler 0x006. Reads the bitmap width, height, origin, move and pointer-flag payload words into the pixel state (+0x0-0x54, +0x34c-0x358) and sets the format fields to fixed values. If the width or height is zero or the data is absent it only advances the raster position: it sends the move words and the handler address from the high half of the state block word +0x4c to the engines and returns. Otherwise it takes the final payload word and branches to `do_bitmap`. |
| `arm_bitmap_dlist` | 0x0c02b590 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2b590). Token handler 0x005, the display-list bitmap. Reads six payload words and the data word, sends the raster-position operands and an engine handler chosen by a mode byte pair (two forms, or the transparent-bitmap setup), sets a mode word in the per-context state to 2, writes the data word masked to 26 bits plus the distributor-continue bit 0x4000000 to the pass-through register at 0x200058, waits for the distributor to drain, then sends the bitmap operand and the closing engine handler address and clears the mode word. |
| `arm_drawpixels` | 0x0c02b9ec | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2b9ec). Token handler 0x03a. Reads the image rectangle, format, type and flags from the host-interface block into the pixel state, takes the final payload word as the data-present flag, clears bit 0x20 of the transfer flags, writes distribution mode 0 through descriptor 0x44000020, sets the operation word to 0x41, programs the drawing buffers, resolves the path, parses the image, calls the path's four set-up slots and streams the pixels, then runs the finish slot (or stores it as a continuation) and restores the flags. |
| `arm_copypixels` | 0x0c02bb34 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2bb34). Token handler 0x038. Reads source x, y, width, height, copy type and the overlap word into the pixel state, hands off to the video-source form when the video flag is set, otherwise queries the overlap of source and destination, writes distribution mode 0 through descriptor 0x44000020, sets drawing buffers for colour copy (type 0x1800) or marks the state dirty, resolves the copy path, waits for the overlap result (printing an error when it demands a read-write copy), sets the raster read-back and the engine slots, starts the raster bus and runs the finish slot. |
| `arm_readpixels` | 0x0c02c3ec | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2c3ec). Token handler 0x039. Reads the rectangle, format, type and pack words, saves and clears bit 0x20 of the transfer flags, and clips a rectangle that reaches past the 4095 limit by writing the shortened sizes to bus registers 0x452-0x456. It sends an engine operand if a select flag is set, then either takes the video form or writes distribution mode 0, resolves the read path, writes raster register 0x011d3fcc through descriptor 0x58404800, sets the read-back, runs the engine slots, sends the pixels to the host and runs the finish slot. |
| `arm_copyisoverlapped` | 0x0c02c94c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2c94c). Token handler 0x110. Reads three payload words and the final word, asks the overlap checker whether a copy source overlaps the destination, waits for the answer, then opens a read-back reply and writes 1 and the answer to bus register 0x476, so the host learns whether the copy would overlap. |
| `arm_copyconvisoverlapped` | 0x0c02c9b0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2c9b0). Token handler 0x115. Reads three payload words and the final word, asks the overlap checker, waits for the answer, then opens a read-back reply, writes 0x10 and the answer to bus register 0x476, writes an engine handler address to port 0x4f4, and closes the reply, so the host learns whether a copy-convolution would overlap. |
| `arm_pixel_nomode` | 0x0c02ca20 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2ca20). Token handler 0x111. Marks the pixel state dirty and reads a mode word and a flag word. If flag bit 30 is set it saves the transfer flags at per-context state +0x714 and the pixel-mode word from +0x6b8 to +0x718, clears the transfer flags and sets +0x6b8 to 0x8421; otherwise it restores both from the saved copies. It then points the unpack and pack record pointers at the shadow's own copies (flag bit 29 clear), the texel-store copies (mode 2), the fixed default records (mode 4) or the fixed display-list unpack record (mode 0x20). |
| `InitPolygonStippleState` | 0x0c02cae4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2cae4). Single branch to the engine-side stipple initialiser, called by the per-context pixel-state initialiser. It exists as a named entry only; the work is the initialiser it jumps to. |
| `invalidate_colormatrix` | 0x0c02cae8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2cae8). Marks the pixel state dirty and stores 0xffff at per-context state +0x6b8, the cached colour-matrix word, so the next use sees it as invalid. Called by the matrix handlers such as the frustum handler. No hardware access. |
| `update_colormatrix` | 0x0c02cb0c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2cb0c). Marks the pixel state dirty and stores the argument at per-context state +0x6b8, the cached colour-matrix word. Called by the matrix handlers. No hardware access. |
| `push_colormatrix` | 0x0c02cb28 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2cb28). Marks the pixel state dirty, then pushes the cached colour-matrix word at per-context state +0x6b8 onto a one-entry stack at +0x6b0 and increments the depth at +0x6ac. If the depth is already 1 it does nothing more. Called by `arm_pushmatrix` in colour-matrix mode. |
| `pop_colormatrix` | 0x0c02cb60 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2cb60). Marks the pixel state dirty, decrements the depth at per-context state +0x6ac and, if it did not go negative, reloads the cached colour-matrix word at +0x6b8 from the stack entry at +0x6b0. Called by `arm_popmatrix` in colour-matrix mode. |
| `InitPixelGlobal` | 0x0c02cc30 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2cc30). Run once from the engine start-up routine. Calls the routine that builds the format table from four static tables, then jumps into the routine that copies engine handler addresses into the two conversion function tables. |
| `InitPixGEState` | 0x0c02cc48 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2cc48). Copies twelve engine handler addresses from the engine handler table (offsets 0x430-0x45c) into two six-entry function tables, one at 0x0404ceb0 for integer-format conversion on draw and one at 0x0404cec8 for conversion on read-back. Leaf routine, reached only by a jump from `InitPixelGlobal`. |
| `__glkonGetPromotedSize` | 0x0c036c20 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x36c20). Table look-up: takes the larger of the second and third arguments as a kernel size, uses entry 0 when that is 8 or more, and returns the word at that index in row (first argument) of the promoted-size table, rows being 32 bytes long. Gives the working size of a convolution kernel; called from four pixel-path routines. |
| `arm_convolutionfilter1d` | 0x0c036c44 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x36c44). Token 0xef handler for one-dimensional convolution kernels. Reads the kernel's width and format words from the host-interface block into the pixel-job record at state-block offset 0x900 and the one-dimensional kernel slot of the context block, requests the promoted size, parses the host image, then (for a kernel that is not empty) compiles the colour stream, sets up the engines and sends the pixels down the pipe, or installs the default kernel when width is zero. Defers the finishing step when the reply flag is set. |
| `arm_convolutionfilter2d` | 0x0c036d78 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x36d78). Token 0xf0 handler for two-dimensional convolution kernels. Reads width, height and format words into the pixel-job record at state-block offset 0x900 and the two-dimensional kernel slot of the context block, takes the promoted size of the larger side, parses the host image and, if the kernel is not empty, compiles the colour stream, sets up the engines and sends the pixels to the pipe; an empty kernel installs the default one instead. |
| `SetupCopyConvolve` | 0x0c036ec8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x36ec8). Prepares the source of a copy-form convolution: converts the framebuffer format to a raster-bus format, stores it in the pixel-job record at offset 0xbc of the record given as the first argument, then tail-calls the routine that sets up a read from the framebuffer. Called by both copy-filter handlers. |
| `arm_copyconvolutionfilter1d` | 0x0c036ee8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x36ee8). Token 0xf2 handler: defines a one-dimensional convolution kernel from the framebuffer. Reads the position and size words, fills the pixel-job record at state-block offset 0x900 and the kernel slot, sets up the read-back and engines, writes raster register 0x011d3fcc through descriptor 0x58404800, starts the raster-bus transfer and ends the convolve; installs the default kernel when the width is zero. |
| `arm_copyconvolutionfilter2d` | 0x0c037018 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x37018). Token 0xf3 handler: defines a two-dimensional convolution kernel from the framebuffer. Reads the position and size words, fills the pixel-job record at state-block offset 0x900 and the two-dimensional kernel slot, sets up the read-back and engines, writes raster register 0x011d3fcc through descriptor 0x58404800, starts the transfer and ends the convolve; installs the default kernel when either side is zero. |
| `arm_separablefilter2d` | 0x0c037150 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x37150). Token 0xf5 handler for separable two-dimensional kernels. Reads the row and column sizes and format words into the pixel-job record at state-block offset 0x900 and the separable-kernel slot of the context block, takes the larger side's promoted size, parses the host image, and for a non-empty kernel compiles the colour stream, sets up the engines and sends the pixels; a second pass handles the other axis, or the default kernel is installed when both are empty. |
| `arm_pixel_transform` | 0x0c0376c4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x376c4). Token 0x135 handler. Stores the transform mode from the host-interface block in the state-block word at offset 0x36c. Mode 0x81c5 re-applies the three convolution bits of the pixel-path flag word at offset 0xc88 for the convolutions whose enable bytes are set and clears bit 0x80000; mode 0x81c6 sets bit 0x80000 if the stage is on and clears the convolution bits; any other mode returns unchanged. |
| `arm_pixel_transform_parameterfv` | 0x0c03776c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x3776c). Token 0xf9 handler. Reads a target and a parameter name, and picks the per-context kernel block for one-dimensional, two-dimensional or separable convolution from the target. Scale name 0x8014 stores four floats at offsets 0x28-0x34 of that block, bias name 0x8015 four at 0x38-0x44; it then sets or clears flag bit 1 (scale) or 2 (bias) in the block's flag word at offset 0x20 depending on whether the values differ from the identity 1.0 or 0. |
| `arm_pixel_transform_parameteriv` | 0x0c03784c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x3784c). Token 0xf8 handler. Reads a target and a parameter name, picks the per-context convolution block for the target, and stores one integer from the last payload word: parameter 0x8013 into offset 0x1c of that block, and for the pixel-transform target two further parameters (0x81c9 up to 0x81ca) into context-block offsets 0x55c-0x560. Other names are ignored; touches no hardware. |

## Pixel path: splitting the data across engines

Stream and line planners that divide an image among the geometry engines.

| Symbol | Address | What it is and does |
|---|---|---|
| `BuildStreamWramModel` | 0x0c026cf4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x26cf4). Fills the seven words of the engine working-memory address model with fixed addresses between 0x80c8 and 0x8df0 (word 6 holds 0x119). The stream-path split routines read these words to place their per-engine buffers. Runs once per context from the pixel-state initialiser. |
| `loadDivModEntry` | 0x0c026d54 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x26d54). Fills one 32-byte divide-modulo record at the address in the first argument for the count in the second argument. Stores the count and zero, then the quotient and remainder of the count taken against six, twelve and twenty-four in turn (the runtime divide routine receives the constant first). Used by `DivModFunc` for large counts. |
| `InitDivModLut` | 0x0c026db0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x26db0). Precomputes the divide-modulo table of 129 records of 32 bytes for counts 0-128, each holding the count, a zero word and three quotient and remainder pairs for the divisors six, twelve and twenty-four. Called once per context from the pixel-state initialiser. The split routines then fetch records by count instead of dividing. |
| `DivModFunc` | 0x0c026e28 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x26e28). Returns a pointer to a record holding a count and three quotient and remainder pairs for the divisors six, twelve and twenty-four. For counts below 129 it points into the precomputed table at 32 bytes per count. Larger counts are computed by the entry loader into the next free slot of a scratch area inside the first argument's record (slot counter at offset 0x68, slots from 0x6c). |
| `CompCommonColor` | 0x0c029d4c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x29d4c). Split stage shared by the colour stream and line paths. Picks the engine format record from the engine type-and-format table by the type and format at pixel-block offsets 0x914-0x91c, sets the lane count (3 or the word at 0x950), the mask and shift fields from the transfer state, and a pixels-per-lane value of 4. It then asks the divide-modulo routine for three records (the pixel count times the record size, four times the count, the count itself), storing them at offsets 0x50-0x58 of its work record at 0x9d4. |
| `CompCommonLum` | 0x0c029e3c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x29e3c). Split stage shared by the luminance stream and line paths. Same set-up as the colour form (engine format record, lane count, masks and shifts), but with one pixel per lane, and only two divide-modulo records: one stored at both offsets 0x50 and 0x54 of the work record at pixel-block offset 0x9d4 and one at 0x58. |
| `CompCommonIndexStream` | 0x0c029f14 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x29f14). Split stage of the colour-index stream path. Takes pixels per packet from the per-path table by the path word at 0x9c0 and fixes the work record at 0x9d4 at 32 pixels per pass. It fills the format record, lane count and masks as the colour form does, calls the divide-modulo routine three times with the pixel count, and sets the buffer size, address and stride words at offsets 0x30-0x48 from the engine working-memory model `wramBuf`. |
| `CompCommonColorStream` | 0x0c02a074 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2a074). Split stage of the colour stream paths (colour and colour short circuit). Returns at once when the dirty word at 0x90c is zero. Otherwise it takes pixels per packet from the per-path table by the word at 0x9c0, chooses a short or long pass by the cached word at 0xc80 (bit test), calls `CompCommonColor`, sizes the buffers from the destination type, and writes the buffer sizes and addresses from the working-memory model into the work record at offsets 0x30-0x48. |
| `ResolveNumGEs` | 0x0c02a1b8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2a1b8). Returns how many engines are needed to cover the first argument lines when the first engine takes the sum of the second and third arguments and each further engine takes the third argument; the result is at least 1. |
| `GetLineDist` | 0x0c02a1e4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2a1e4). Splits a range of image lines across engines for the line-at-a-time paths. Looks up the kernel overlap and extra rows from the convolution kernel table (or 3 and 0 when no convolution enable bit 0xe0 is set), computes lines per engine from the division and rounds it up to a multiple of the packet size, and writes the per-engine line counts and sizes into the record in its sixth argument. It returns the engine count, capped by the engine-count global at data offset 0x54. |
| `CompLineDist` | 0x0c02a3f0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2a3f0). Split stage of the line-at-a-time paths. Takes pixels per packet from the per-path table (doubled for one wide format), calls `GetLineDist` with the kernel size, copies the resulting line counts into three work records, then sets the working-memory base from the engine working-memory model and a buffer length derived from 846 divided by a value from the line distributor, forced to an odd number. Called by the colour and luminance line split routines. |
| `__initlinemodedist` | 0x0c02a530 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2a530). Start-up precomputation of the line-mode limits. For engine counts 1-7, three kernel types, five pixels-per-packet values and three raster packet sizes it probes `GetLineDist` for increasing line counts against a budget of 846 words and records the lowest violating count (minus 1) in two limit tables, the second only for raster packet size 0. It finally copies entry 2 over entry 0 of each table. The read-back handler for the table sends these limits to the host. |
| `NumEolGeneratedBytes` | 0x0c02a858 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2a858). Leaf routine used by the line-at-a-time splitters. Returns 0 unless the convolution bits (0xe0) of the transfer flags at pixel state +0x388 are set. Otherwise it takes a line width from the pixel state (the difference of two bounds plus one, chosen by bit 1 or bit 2 of the operation word at +8), scales it by eight, four or two according to a component-size mode, adds two for an odd width in the two-byte mode, and returns the number of filler bytes (zero to seven) that round the line up to a multiple of eight. **Inferred:** these are the bytes the engines append at the end of each convolved line, from the name and the use in the output size. |
| `CompCommonColorLine` | 0x0c02a910 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2a910). Split stage of path 2 (colour, one line at a time, used by convolution), reached through the path table. Returns at once if the transfer-active word at pixel state +0xc is zero. Otherwise it calls the line-distribution splitter and the shared colour splitter, then fills the input descriptor block at pixel state +0xd4 and the output block at +0x200 with line sizes in 4-byte words (forced odd), buffer addresses taken from the engine working-memory model, line strides, the end-of-line padding from `NumEolGeneratedBytes`, three quotients at +0x50-0x58 from the divide helper, and the format record pointers. |
| `ConvolveReduce` | 0x0c02aa90 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2aa90). Size correction after a convolution stage: takes a descriptor block and a component count, subtracts the kernel border (word +4) from the width (word +0), then stores two divide-helper results in the block, at +0x54 (new width times the component count) and +0x58 (new width). Called by the colour path loader with 4 components and the luminance path loader with 1, so the module chain downstream sees the narrower output. |
| `PadGeVOutcomp` | 0x0c02aad0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2aad0). Pads the output size of a stage to whole engine packets: reads the count at the word that block +0x58 points to, rounds it down to a multiple of the packet unit held in the state block at +0xc3c using the signed divide, and returns if that is already at least the count. Otherwise it adds one packet unit and rewrites the block's two size entries at +0x58 and +0x54 (the second multiplied by the component count in the second argument) through the divide helper. Called when the transfer is a line-mode convolution. |
| `CompCommonLumStream` | 0x0c02ab34 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2ab34). Split stage of the luminance, depth and short-circuit stream paths (the second, sixth and eighth records of the path table). Sets the stream size word to 0x20 in the input block at pixel state +0xd4 and the output block at +0x32c, looks up the packet size in the per-format table, calls the shared luminance splitter, then fills the word count (forced odd), the strides and the buffer addresses at +0x30-0x44 from the engine working-memory model buffer addresses. |
| `CompCommon_FFT` | 0x0c02abec | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2abec). Split stage of path 9, the FFT transfer: clamps the width at pixel state +0 to 0x100, stores it as the size in both descriptor blocks (+0xd4 and +0x32c), and points the input block's format pointer at the record in the format table selected by the type (+0x1c) and component (+0x14) fields. Leaf routine, returns without writing hardware. |
| `CompCommonLumLine` | 0x0c02ac30 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2ac30). Split stage of path 3 (luminance, one line at a time, used by convolution). Returns if the transfer-active word at pixel state +0xc is zero. Otherwise it calls the line-distribution splitter and the shared luminance splitter and fills the input block at +0xd4 and the output block at +0x200: line size in words (forced odd), strides, buffer base from the working-memory model, the end-of-line padding from `NumEolGeneratedBytes`, and sizes at +0x50-0x58 from the divide helper. |
| `AddDispatch` | 0x0c02f82c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2f82c). Writes a four-word block (two zeros, the state-block word at 0x904 minus one, and that word modulo three) to engine control memory 0x16a through the control-memory writer; it appends nothing to a module list. The colour and luminance line set-ups call it. |

## Pixel path: engine pipeline modules

The engine-side pixel pipeline: the modules each stage adds and their parameters.

| Symbol | Address | What it is and does |
|---|---|---|
| `init_pg` | 0x0c0037ac | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x37ac). Programs the pixel-generator chips on the raster boards through raster-register writes: descriptor 0x58404800 with address words 0x01270800-0x012b0806, and descriptor 0x584fc800 with address words 0x01253be0-0x01263be1, using constants such as 0x8008; it then writes 1 to board register 0x3ff6 as a tail call. `init_rm_board` runs it. |
| `init_tf` | 0x0c00386c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x386c). Programs the texture-filter chips: a raster-register write of 0 to address word 0x01661410, then of 0x4000 to 0x0160140e, both through descriptor 0x58404800; a board-register write to 0x1409; and a final write of a second constant through the word-write routine for board register 0. `init_rm_board` runs it. |
| `init_tg` | 0x0c0038e8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x38e8). Writes five raster registers through descriptor 0x58404800, address words 0x01203fcf-0x01243fd3, with small constants (the largest is 0xf00f); it uses no engine handler and returns by hand. `init_rm_board` runs it. |
| `init_tm` | 0x0c003980 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x3980). Counts, from the identification words of eight chips per raster board in the configuration block, how many carry generation nibble 2 and how many 3. When every chip is of the same generation and the board type is 1 or a type from 3 to 5, it writes board registers 0x1023 (value one) and 0x5004 (value zero); when the generations are mixed on those board types it prints a warning about down-revision chips. **Inferred:** the chips are the texture-memory chips, from the routine's name. |
| `init_imp` | 0x0c003aac | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x3aac). Programs the image-processor chips: clears board register 0x1408, writes a dozen image-processor registers through descriptor 0x58404800 (address words 0x01351c02-0x013e1c15), counts the chips of the image-processor type per board and records a small mode number in the state-block byte at offset 0xcf1 from the chip count and board type, and writes 0x30000000 to register 0x013c1c0d for the all-matching case. |
| `init_rm_board` | 0x0c003de8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x3de8). Start-up step for the raster boards: unless simulated it runs the scan-chain set-up, then calls the pixel-generator, texture-filter, texture-generator, texture-memory and image-processor routines in turn, and prints a warning when the texture-filter chips are an old revision. In the simulator it prints a skip message and returns when the word at RAM 0x103c is non-zero, and otherwise reads the raster-board count from RAM 0x1048. |
| `InitModulePath` | 0x0c02ccc4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2ccc4). Empties a module list: writes zero to the entry count at word 0 and to the five tail fields at +0xe4-0xf4. Each loader calls it on its stack list before appending conversion modules, which `WriteCram` later writes to engine control memory address 0x129. |
| `ReloadVolatileConvState` | 0x0c02cd3c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2cd3c). Re-sends convolution state the engines lose between line-mode passes. If the per-context word at +0x71c is nonzero it uses that value as an engine external memory address and writes the one word at +0x720 there; then it tail-calls the control-memory writer to write six words from +0x724 to control memory address 0x112. Called by the colour-line and luminance-line stages when the convolution bits (0xe0) of the transfer flags are set and no line pass runs. |
| `loadColorPath` | 0x0c02d61c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2d61c). Builds the module list for a colour pixel transfer. After emptying the list it appends the input stage (complex or expand-to-RGBA), then in order the stages the transfer flags in pixel state +0x388 request: scale and bias, colour lookup, convolution (plain or five-tap separable, followed by the size correction), colour matrix or post-matrix scale and bias, further lookups, histogram, min-max, sum to luminance, float-to-integer conversion, padding and the output stage. It ends with one of two next-engine handlers, chosen by engine count and packet counts, and writes the list to control memory address 0x129. |
| `loadIndexPath` | 0x0c02d980 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2d980). Builds the module list for a colour-index transfer. It appends float-to-integer conversion if the input type needs it, then (for index or shift modes in the transfer flags) the shift-and-add module, which for a shift outside the supported range also writes a six-word parameter block to control memory 0x1b0 first, then the index lookup when its flag bit is set, then the colour-index output stage, then a next-engine handler chosen by engine count and packet counts, and writes the list to control memory address 0x129. |
| `AddOutputI` | 0x0c02dbe8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2dbe8). Appends the output stage of a colour-index or stencil transfer. Picks one output-module record from the colour-index table by the destination plane mask (or the bitmap pair, the pop-up plane record, the normal or overlay 8-bit or 12-bit record from the mode bytes, or the stencil record), adds int-to-float conversion when the input needs it, writes the record's two parameter words to control memory 0x1c4 and four words to 0x166, calls the record's builder routine and appends one more handler word read from the shadow or the engine handler table. |
| `loadStencilPath` | 0x0c02dd90 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2dd90). Builds the module list for a stencil transfer: float-to-integer conversion if the input type needs it, the shift-and-add module when bits 0x18 of the transfer flags are set, the index lookup when bit 0x40000 is set, the stencil flavour of the index output stage, then a next-engine handler chosen by engine count and packet counts, and writes the list to control memory address 0x129. |
| `loadLuminancePath` | 0x0c02df18 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2df18). Builds the module list for a luminance transfer. It appends the input stage (complex or one-component), then the one-component forms of the stages the transfer flags request (scale and bias, lookup, convolution with the size correction, post-matrix scale and bias, histogram, min-max), float-to-integer conversion, padding and the one-component output stage, then a next-engine handler chosen by engine count and packet counts, and writes the list to control memory address 0x129. |
| `loadDepthPath` | 0x0c02e26c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2e26c). Builds the module list for a depth transfer: decompress packed depth (format 6) and switch the input format pointer to the unpacked record, append the one-component input stage, a scale and bias when the depth scale is not 1.0 or the bias is not 0, a float-to-integer stage when the type needs it, the one-component output stage, then a next-engine handler chosen by engine count and packet counts, and writes the list to control memory address 0x129. |
| `loadShortCircuitPath` | 0x0c02e3f4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2e3f4). Builds the module list for a short-circuit transfer, where data goes from input to output with no conversion. It selects an output record from the short-circuit table by format and flag bit 0x80, writes its two parameter words to control memory 0x1c4 and four to 0x166, calls its builder, appends one handler word, then a next-engine handler chosen by engine count and packet counts, and writes the list to control memory address 0x129. |
| `loadColorTablePath` | 0x0c02e55c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2e55c). Builds the module list for a colour-table load: the expand-to-RGBA input stage, a scale and bias unless the table's scale and bias words (read from the pixel shadow) are 1.0 and 0, the table-output stage taking the third argument, one more handler word from the engine handler table, and writes the list to control memory address 0x129. |
| `loadBitmapPath` | 0x0c02e640 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2e640). Builds the module list for a bitmap transfer from the one fixed output record at 0x0404c9fc: writes its two parameter words to control memory 0x1c4 and four to 0x166, calls its builder, appends one handler word (from the mode bytes or the handler table), a next-engine handler chosen by engine count and packet counts, and writes the list to control memory address 0x129. |
| `loadInputExpandRGBA` | 0x0c02e7b0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2e7b0). Appends the input stage that turns packed host pixels into four floats. With the colour-table flag bits (0x1800) it adds an optional line copy, float-to-integer, shift-and-add and index lookup. For a four-component 8-bit-style packed format it adds the extract module, a divide-helper size and int-to-float with signed bias. Otherwise it adds copy-float, or unsigned shift, int-to-float and signed bias as the type needs. Sets the list's last-stage code and the pointers into the descriptor block. |
| `loadInput1Cmpnt` | 0x0c02eb10 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2eb10). Appends the input stage for a one-component transfer and clears the separable-kernel flag in the conversion parameters. Without convolution it adds copy-float for a float source, or an unsigned shift, int-to-float and signed bias for an integer source, and records last-stage code 5 or 6 in the list. With convolution flags set, a matching per-context table value of 10 sets that flag and returns; otherwise it adds a line copy, an optional shift and int-to-float. Called by the luminance and depth path loaders. |
| `loadComplexInput` | 0x0c02ecfc | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2ecfc). Appends the input stage for complex (FFT) data and clears the separable-kernel flag. For a float source it adds only the shift-real-FIFO module and returns. For an integer source needing a sign bias it adds the scale-real-FIFO module and signed bias and records last-stage code 5; otherwise it adds the scale-real-FIFO module (or a shift-real-FIFO plus int-to-float) and records code 6. Called by `loadColorPath` and `loadLuminancePath` when the format has complex-data bits (0x1fc000). |
| `__glkonSetDefaultKernel` | 0x0c02ede0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2ede0). Loads the default convolution kernel into engine external memory: for the kernel number in the first argument it looks up the size from the kernel record, then calls `WriteEram` to copy `defaultKernel` to the kernel's external-memory address (the separable kernel number 2 gets two consecutive copies, one per axis). The convolution-filter token handlers and the slow-state initialiser call it. |
| `LoadKernel` | 0x0c02ee5c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2ee5c). Prepares a convolution kernel for a line-mode transfer: it first saves the engine control-memory region that the kernel will overwrite into engine external memory (setting a restore flag at offset 0x648 of the current context), then sends four strided copies with `mgeb_StrideEramToCram` that move the kernel from its external-memory address into control memory, and records the control-memory address in the kernel record at offset 0x48. Called by the colour and luminance line set-ups. |
| `RestoreKernel` | 0x0c02efbc | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2efbc). If the restore flag at offset 0x648 of the current context is set, copies the engine control-memory region that the kernel loader saved back from engine external memory and clears the flag; otherwise it returns at once. Its only caller is the pixel-end routine at 0x0c026a68, when bits 0xe0 of the state-block word at 0xc88 are set. |
| `AddHipSetter` | 0x0c02f018 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2f018). Appends one module to the transfer chain: it calls `mmo_SetupHip` to write the module's parameters, then adds the engine handler for `the control word 0` and the module list passed as the second argument to the module list passed as the second argument. Called by the colour and luminance path builders. |
| `AddShiftAdd` | 0x0c02f060 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2f060). Appends a shift-and-add module to the transfer chain: it calls `U_uShiftAdd` with the state-block words at 0x2b0 (plus the extra stack argument) and 0x2b4, then adds the handler for `mmo_ShiftAdd` and the parameter address 0x1a3 to the module list passed as the second argument. |
| `AddIndexLut` | 0x0c02f0c8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2f0c8). Appends the colour-index look-up stage: when the context's table-present word is zero it adds a zero scale-and-bias module and records kind 6; for the first two destination formats it calls the colour-index table writer and adds the index-to-index module; otherwise it calls that writer with four table sizes, adds the index-to-colour module, then an integer-to-float module, and records kind 5 at offset 0xf4 of the module list. |
| `AddUnsignedIntShift` | 0x0c02f24c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2f24c). Appends a shift module for unsigned integer data: it calls `U_uShiftAdd` with offset -1 and add 0, then adds the handler for `mmo_ShiftAdd` and the parameter address 0x1a3 to the module list passed as the second argument. |
| `AddExtract` | 0x0c02f2a8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2f2a8). Appends the unpack module for a packed twelve-bit-colour four-bit-alpha format: it calls `U_uExtract` to write the shift and mask table, then adds the handler for `mmo_ExtractL12A4` with the control word 0 to the module list passed as the second argument. |
| `AddDecompressZ` | 0x0c02f2ec | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2f2ec). Appends the depth-decompression module: it calls `U_uDecompress` to write its four parameter words, then adds the handler for `mmo_DecompressZ16` with the control word 0 to the module list passed as the second argument. Only the depth path builder calls it. |
| `AddCopyFloat` | 0x0c02f330 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2f330). Appends a copy stage for floating-point source data: it calls `U_uShiftAdd` with zero offset and zero add, so the data passes unchanged, then adds the handler for `mmo_ShiftAdd` and the parameter address 0x1a3 to the module list passed as the second argument. |
| `AddShiftRealFifo` | 0x0c02f390 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2f390). Appends the shift module for real data entering the complex transform path: it writes a five-word block (the transfer's offset words, the bytes per pixel and a shift argument) to engine external memory 0xbfc, then adds the handler for `mmo_Shift_Skip` with parameter address 0xbfc to the module list passed as the second argument. |
| `AddI2FScaleRealFifo` | 0x0c02f43c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2f43c). Appends the integer-to-float scale module for real data entering the complex transform path: it writes a five-word block to engine external memory 0xbfc (the last word is a scale constant picked by the source data type), then adds the handler for `mmo_I2F_Scale_Skip` with parameter address 0xbfc to the module list passed as the second argument. |
| `AddCopyFifoLine` | 0x0c02f4f8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2f4f8). Appends a line-copy module: it calls `U_mCopyFifoLine`, adds the handler for `mmo_CopyFifoLine` with control word 0 to the module list passed as the second argument, then copies word 0x34 of the transfer record over word 0x30. |
| `AddInt2Float` | 0x0c02f548 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2f548). Appends an integer-to-float conversion module: it calls `U_uI2F` to write the parameters, adds the handler for `mmo_IntToFloat`, and gives it the next parameter address from the table at the start of the integer-to-float parameter block (the counter at offset 0xe4 of the module list advances). |
| `AddSignedBias` | 0x0c02f5c0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2f5c0). Appends a signed bias stage: it calls `U_uSignScaleBias` with the scale-bias counter at offset 0xf0 of the module list, then adds the handler for `mmo_ScaleBias` and the next scale-bias parameter address (the counter advances). |
| `AddF2I` | 0x0c02f624 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2f624). Appends a float-to-integer conversion module: it calls `U_uF2I`, which writes six words to engine control memory 0x1ba, then adds the handler for `mmo_FloatToInt` with parameter address 0x1ba to the module list passed as the second argument. |
| `AddF2ISignScaleBias` | 0x0c02f66c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2f66c). Appends a signed scale-and-bias stage for the float-to-integer step: it takes the bias table from the integer-to-float parameter block (96 bytes per format index), calls `U_uSignScaleBias`, then adds the handler for `mmo_ScaleBias` and the next scale-bias parameter address; despite the name it converts nothing itself. |
| `AddExpand2RGBA` | 0x0c02f6d4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2f6d4). Appends the module that expands source data to four colour components: it calls `U_uExpand`, which writes ten words to engine control memory 0x1b0, then adds the handler for `mmo_Expand` with parameter address 0x1b1 to the module list passed as the second argument. |
| `AddScaleBias` | 0x0c02f720 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2f720). Appends a scale-and-bias module: it calls `U_uScaleBias` with the counter at offset 0xf0 of the module list, then adds the handler for `mmo_ScaleBias` and the next scale-bias parameter address (the counter advances). Four-component scale and bias arrays are the third and fourth arguments. |
| `AddScaleBias1Cmpnt` | 0x0c02f778 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2f778). Appends a one-component scale-and-bias module: it calls `U_uScaleBias1Cmpnt` with the counter at offset 0xf0 of the module list, then adds the handler for `mmo_ScaleBias` and the next scale-bias parameter address (the counter advances). |
| `AddClamp` | 0x0c02f7d0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2f7d0). Appends a clamp module: it calls `U_uClamp` with the counter at offset 0xe8 of the module list, then adds the handler for `mmo_Clamp` and the next clamp parameter address (the counter advances). |
| `AddRGBALut` | 0x0c02f87c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2f87c). Appends the look-up table stage for four-component colour: nothing happens when the table size is zero; otherwise it adds a clamp module from the context's bounds, calls `U_uLut`, adds `mmo_LUT16` (or the large-table variant when the table exceeds 0x1000 entries), then an integer-to-float module. |
| `AddLuminanceLut` | 0x0c02f9e4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2f9e4). Appends the look-up table stage for luminance data: nothing happens when the table size is zero; otherwise it adds a clamp module whose four bounds are all one value, calls `U_uLut` with zero table offsets, adds `mmo_LUT16`, then an integer-to-float module. |
| `__glkonFixConvKernDirection` | 0x0c02fad8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2fad8). Flips a stored convolution kernel in engine external memory so that its orientation matches the requested one: it compares the requested direction word (offset 0x64 of the first argument) with the kernel record's stored direction, sends `mgeb_FlipXConvKern`, the vertical-flip handler, or both with operands built from `ConvolveEramLoc`, then stores the new direction. Called by the line set-ups and the two filter read-back handlers. |
| `AddConvolution` | 0x0c02fc54 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2fc54). Appends the convolution module for the kernel shape selected by the word at offset 0x570 of the kernel record: it indexes `UConvolveTable`, calls that entry's parameter writer, then adds the entry's engine handler (a packed table slot below 0x80, else a handler-table word) with control word 0 to the module list passed as the third argument. |
| `AddFastSep5Convolution` | 0x0c02fcf8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2fcf8). Appends the fast separable five-tap convolution module: it calls `U_uFastConv5x5S`, then adds the handler for `mmo_FastSepConvolve5x5_124` with control word 0 to the module list passed as the second argument. The colour and luminance path builders call it. |
| `AddColorMatrix` | 0x0c02fd44 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2fd44). Appends the colour-matrix module: it calls `U_mColorMatrix` to write the matrix, then adds the handler for `mmo_ColorMatrix` with control word 0 to the module list passed as the second argument. Only the colour path builder calls it. |
| `AddPostClrMtrxScaleBias` | 0x0c02fd8c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2fd8c). Appends the scale-and-bias stage that follows the colour matrix: it calls `U_uPostClrMtrxScaleBias`, then adds the handler for `mmo_ScaleBias` with the fixed parameter address 0xbd5 to the module list passed as the second argument. |
| `AddPostClrMtrxScaleBias1Cmpnt` | 0x0c02fddc | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2fddc). Appends the one-component scale-and-bias stage that follows the colour matrix: it calls `U_uPostClrMtrxScaleBias1Cmpnt`, then adds the handler for `mmo_ScaleBias` with the fixed parameter address 0xbd5 to the module list passed as the second argument. |
| `AddHistogram` | 0x0c02fe2c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2fe2c). Appends the histogram module only when the histogram record's enable word at offset 0x524 of the current context is nonzero: it calls `U_uHistogram`, then adds the handler for `mmo_Histogram` with parameter address 0x174 to the module list passed as the second argument. |
| `AddMinMax` | 0x0c02fe88 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2fe88). Appends two modules for the min-max query: it calls `U_uMinMax` to write seven words to engine control memory 0x17c, adds the min-max engine handler with that address, then adds the module that copies the results from control memory to external memory. |
| `AddCompOrder` | 0x0c02fef8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2fef8). Appends the module that reverses the order of the four colour components: it calls `U_uCompOrder` to write three words to engine external memory 0xbae, then adds the component-order reversal handler with that address. The output-module chooser calls it when bit 0x100 of the format word is set. |
| `AddSumRGB2L` | 0x0c02ff40 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2ff40). Appends the module that sums the three colour components into one luminance value: it calls `U_uSumRGB2L` to write five words to engine external memory 0xbb1, then adds the handler for `mmo_SumRGB2L` with that address. The colour path builder calls it. |
| `AddArbClamp` | 0x0c02ff88 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2ff88). Appends the clamp module with arbitrary bounds: it calls its parameter writer to write four words to engine external memory 0xbbc (including the bounds at state-block offsets 0x934-0x938), then adds the arbitrary-clamp engine handler with that address. Only the convolution read-back path uses it. |
| `AddCopyEramToWram` | 0x0c030540 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x30540). Appends the module that copies a convolution result from engine external memory into working memory: it calls `U_uCopyEramToWram` with the kernel record, then adds the handler for `mmo_ConvEramToWram` with parameter address 0xbb6. Only the convolution read-back path uses it. |
| `loadConvolutionReadPath` | 0x0c0305e8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x305e8). Builds the module chain for reading a convolution result back: starting from `InitModulePath` it adds the eram-to-working-memory copy, an arbitrary clamp and signed scale-and-bias unless the format is a plain one, the output module, and finally `mmo_PixStreamNextGE` (or its workaround variant when a word at 0x04067f30 exceeds two), then writes the chain to engine control memory 0x129. Called by the convolution set-up. |
| `loadConvolutionPath` | 0x0c03073c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x3073c). Builds the module chain for a convolution transfer: starting from `InitModulePath` it adds the input conversion stages for the source format (copy, shift, integer-to-float, signed bias, expand), an optional scale and bias, the convolution output module and `mmo_PixStreamBrdcastCore0ANextGE`, then writes the chain to engine control memory 0x129. |
| `loadPolygonStipplePath` | 0x0c0308cc | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x308cc). Builds the module chain for polygon-stipple loading: starting from an empty chain it adds the stipple output module and the broadcast next-engine module, then writes the two-entry chain to engine control memory 0x129. The polygon-stipple set-up calls it. |
| `U_uExtract` | 0x0c03092c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x3092c). Writes the parameter block of the twelve-bit-colour four-bit-alpha unpack module to engine external memory 0xbc0 (21 words: the transfer offsets, bytes per pixel and shift in bits, then sixteen shift and mask words from a data table); if no pending record exists it also stores 0xbc4 and the first parameter word at offsets 0xc04-0xc08 of the integer-to-float block. The unpack-module appender is its only caller. |
| `U_uF2I` | 0x0c0309cc | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x309cc). Writes six words to engine control memory 0x1ba for the float-to-integer module: the transfer's offset word, half of word 0x38, word 0x34, two words from the destination format record and a conversion word taken from the integer-to-float parameter block. `AddF2I` is the only caller. |
| `U_uDecompress` | 0x0c030a34 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x30a34). Writes four words to engine external memory 0xbf2 for the depth-decompression module: the transfer's offset word, the bytes per pixel, and the next two words of the transfer record (offsets 0x34-0x38). `AddDecompressZ` is the only caller. |
| `U_uHipSetter` | 0x0c030a80 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x30a80). Writes a four-word block to engine external memory 0xdc6: two addresses taken from the state block (words at 0xca8-0xcac when the flag at 0xca4 is set, else 0xc98-0xc9c plus 8), then the first argument and the first argument plus a state-block word. `AddHipSetter` is the only caller. |
| `U_uShiftAdd` | 0x0c030ae8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x30ae8). Writes seven words to engine control memory 0x1a3 for the shift-and-add module: the transfer's offset word, half the stack argument, the bytes per pixel, the leftover bits, and the three offset, add and shift arguments. `AddShiftAdd`, `AddUnsignedIntShift` and the copy stage call it. |
| `U_mCopyFifoLine` | 0x0c030b4c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x30b4c). Writes five words to engine external memory 0xbfc for the line-copy module (the transfer offset, bytes per pixel, leftover bits, word 0x34 and half of word 0x38), and records 0xc00 at offset 0xc04 of `int2FloatParams` when nothing is recorded yet. `AddCopyFifoLine` is the only caller. |
| `U_uI2F` | 0x0c030bd0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x30bd0). Writes six words to the engine external-memory address taken from the start of the integer-to-float parameter block, indexed by the counter argument: the data word, two words of the format record, two destination-record words and a scale constant picked by the data type; it also records the address at offset 0xc04 of `int2FloatParams` if none is recorded. Called by `AddInt2Float`. |
| `U_uCopyEramToWram` | 0x0c030c60 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x30c60). Writes six words to engine external memory 0xbb6 for the convolution copy module: the transfer's word 0x38, the kernel's external-memory address, a size and an offset computed from the kernel record (the separable kernel number 2 takes a different layout), the record's word 0x8 and a read-format word from `convIntFmtReadFcn`. `AddCopyEramToWram` is its only caller. |
| `U_uSignScaleBias` | 0x0c030d10 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x30d10). Writes a 0x1d-word block to the scale-bias parameter address chosen by the counter argument: the offset argument twice, zero, two words of the destination record and 24 words copied from the bias table argument; it also records the address in the integer-to-float block if none is recorded. Called by the signed-bias appenders. |
| `U_uExpand` | 0x0c030d9c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x30d9c). Writes ten words to engine control memory 0x1b0 for the expand-to-four-components module: the layout fields depend on the source format (a jump table over format 0-7), selecting the component positions, widths and the fill word taken from the integer-to-float parameter block. `AddExpand2RGBA` is its only caller. |
| `U_uScaleBias` | 0x0c030fa4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x30fa4). Writes a 0x1d-word block to the scale-bias parameter address chosen by the counter argument: header words from the transfer record, then the four component scale values and four bias values, each triple repeated three times. `AddScaleBias` is its only caller; it also records the address at offset 0xc04 of `int2FloatParams` if none is recorded. |
| `U_uScaleBias1Cmpnt` | 0x0c0310a4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x310a4). Writes a 0x1d-word block like the four-component scale-and-bias writer, but every component uses element 0 of the scale array and of the bias array, and the second header word is zero. `AddScaleBias1Cmpnt` is its only caller. |
| `U_uPostClrMtrxScaleBias1Cmpnt` | 0x0c031178 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x31178). Writes a 0x1d-word scale-and-bias block to the fixed engine external-memory address 0xbd5 for the one-component stage after the colour matrix; every component uses element 0 of the two array arguments. `AddPostClrMtrxScaleBias1Cmpnt` is its only caller. |
| `U_uClamp` | 0x0c03124c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x3124c). Writes 17 words to the clamp parameter address chosen by the counter argument: header words from the transfer record, then the four lower and four upper bounds repeated, taken from the array argument or 1.0 when it is null. `AddClamp` is its only caller. |
| `U_uLut` | 0x0c0312d4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x312d4). Writes 14 words to the look-up table parameter address chosen by the counter argument: header words from the transfer record, the table size argument and the first six table offset words. Called by `AddRGBALut` and `AddLuminanceLut`. |
| `U_mLutCI` | 0x0c031360 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x31360). Writes 16 words to engine external memory 0xb9e for the colour-index look-up module: words of the transfer record, the table sizes (four from the second argument, four more from a stack argument) and the table address; it also records 0xb9e at offset 0xc04 of `int2FloatParams` if none is recorded. `AddIndexLut` is its only caller. |
| `U_uCompOrder` | 0x0c031424 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x31424). Writes three words to engine external memory 0xbae for the component-reversal module: word 0x34 of the transfer record, the pixel width in bits shifted right by five and its remainder modulo 32. `AddCompOrder` is the only caller. |
| `U_uSumRGB2L` | 0x0c031470 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x31470). Writes five words to engine external memory 0xbb1 for the module that sums three colour components into luminance: word 0x44 of the transfer record, the source pixel width shifted right by five and its remainder modulo 32. `AddSumRGB2L` is the only caller. |
| `U_uArbClamp` | 0x0c0314bc | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x314bc). Writes four words to engine external memory 0xbbc for the arbitrary-bound clamp module: word 0x34 of the transfer record, the pixel width shifted right by five, and the lower and upper bounds from state-block offsets 0x934-0x938. `AddArbClamp` is its only caller. |
| `U_uHistogram` | 0x0c03150c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x3150c). Writes eight words to engine control memory 0x174 for the histogram module: 0x2a8, the argument, words 0x10-0x14 of the histogram record, word 0x44 of the transfer record, the destination width and word 0x18 of the record. `AddHistogram` is its only caller. |
| `U_uMinMax` | 0x0c03156c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x3156c). Writes seven words to engine control memory 0x17c for the min-max module: the argument, word 0x44 of the transfer record, two destination-record words and two zeros and 1.0 for the initial range. `AddMinMax` is its only caller. |
| `U_mColorMatrix` | 0x0c031690 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x31690). Writes five words to engine control memory 0x18b (the first argument, -1 and word 0x44 of the transfer record) and eight words to engine external memory 0xbda that interleave the words of the two matrix arrays. `AddColorMatrix` is its only caller. |
| `U_uPostClrMtrxScaleBias` | 0x0c03172c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x3172c). Writes a 0x1d-word scale-and-bias block to the fixed engine external-memory address 0xbd5 for the four-component stage after the colour matrix, interleaving the two array arguments. `AddPostClrMtrxScaleBias` is its only caller. |
| `SetupGE_PerRect` | 0x0c031bc4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x31bc4). Per-rectangle engine step that ends every pixel-transfer set-up: it writes the component-layout code from `compFormatTable` and a handler sent from the state block, sends the last-engine counter set-up handler with 0x7fffffff, and (unless a line transfer in the second or third mode) writes descriptor 0x58400800 with the filler 0x2bad2dad once per engine, then repeats the sends. Every stream and line set-up routine ends by jumping to it. |
| `UConvolveTable` | 0x0404d3a8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` 6.5.30m). Twelve pairs of an engine module slot (0x11b-0x11d) and one of three writer routines in the image (from 0x0c032080 to 0x0c0325b0), one pair per kernel shape; `AddConvolution` picks the pair that builds the convolution stage. |

## Pixel path: finishing a transfer

Sending the data, draining and restoring state after a transfer.

| Symbol | Address | What it is and does |
|---|---|---|
| `CommonPixEnd` | 0x0c026654 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x26654). Shared tail of the pixel-transfer finish routines. Clears dirty bits 0-8 of the flag word at pixel-block offset 0x90c. When bit 0x4 of the path word at 0x908 is set it writes tbus register 0x1415 and then nine raster registers (0x011d3fcc-0x014a0c04, among them the texture-filter bind registers 0x01483fd5 and 0x01493fd4); when bit 0x40 is set and the path format field at 0x9c0 is 0x19 it re-sends the stencil reference and depth-test raster registers from state-block words 0x6fc-0x76d. When bit 0x20 is set it clears raster register 0x01100441. Finally it clears 0x015c1500, stores the low half of the word at 0xc0 to port 0x4f8 and an engine handler address from the engine-handler table to port 0x4f4. It is called by the stream finish and the line finish. |
| `PixEndRGBStream` | 0x0c0269b0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x269b0). Finish stage of the colour, luminance, short-circuit, index, depth and stencil stream paths. Calls the shared finish routine, then, when bit 0x40 of the path word is set, a flag in the word at 0x920 is set and the framebuffer format at context-record offset 0x650 is 3, writes 1 through descriptor 0x584fc800 to raster register 0x0167140f. It then tail-jumps to the distributor-mode restorer. |
| `PixEnd_FFT` | 0x0c026a14 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x26a14). Finish stage of the FFT pixel path. Clears dirty bits 0-8 of the flag word at 0x90c, writes zero to raster register 0x015c1500 through descriptor 0x58404800, stores the low half of the word at 0xc0 to port 0x4f8 and an engine handler address from table offset 0x638 to port 0x4f4, then tail-jumps to the distributor-mode restorer. |
| `PixEndRGBLine` | 0x0c026a68 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x26a68). Finish stage of the line-at-a-time paths used for convolution. When any convolution enable bit (0xe0) of the flag word at 0xc88 is set it first restores the convolution kernel in engine memory. It then runs the shared finish routine, sends an engine handler address from table offset 0x2c0 to port 0x4f4 once per engine, and tail-jumps to the distributor-mode restorer. **Inferred:** the repeat count is the engine count, from the global used as the engine cap in the line distributor. |
| `BitmapEndStream` | 0x0c026ac4 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x26ac4). Finish stage for bitmap drawing. Clears dirty bits 0-8 of the flag word at 0x90c, stores the low half of the word at 0xc0 to port 0x4f8 and an engine handler address from table offset 0x638 to port 0x4f4, passes the three words at pixel-block offsets 0x350-0x358 to the data port and the word at 0x34c to port 0x478, then starts the engine handler whose address is the high half of the state word at 0x4c. It ends by tail-jumping to the distributor-mode restorer. |
| `PixEndReadConvolve` | 0x0c026bdc | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x26bdc). Finish stage for reading a convolution filter back to the host. Writes zero to raster register 0x01100441 with select 0x7f through descriptor 0x58404800, stores the low half of the word at 0xc0 to port 0x4f8 and an engine handler address from table offset 0x638 to port 0x4f4, then tail-jumps to the distributor-mode restorer. |
| `PixEndConvolve` | 0x0c026c1c | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x26c1c). Finish stage for loading a convolution filter (one-dimensional, two-dimensional, copy and separable forms). Writes zero to raster register 0x015c1500 through descriptor 0x58404800, stores the high half of the word at 0xc0 to port 0x4f8 and an engine handler address from table offset 0x638 to port 0x4f4, sends a zero value through descriptor 0x58a00020, and tail-jumps to the distributor-mode restorer. |
| `PixEndPolygonStipple` | 0x0c026c68 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x26c68). Finish stage for loading the polygon stipple. Stores the high half of the word at 0xc0 to port 0x4f8, sends a zero value through descriptor 0x58a00020, zeroes the two rotation words at context-record offsets 0x10c0-0x10c4, calls the rotation routine to resend the rotation, and tail-jumps to the distributor-mode restorer. |
| `arm_clearaccum` | 0x0c037d94 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x37d94). Token 0x1b handler. Reads four floats from the host-interface block and stores them as the accumulation-clear colour at state-block offsets 0x6ec-0x6f8. Sends nothing to hardware; the stored colour is used when the accumulation buffer is cleared. |
| `arm_accum` | 0x0c037dd0 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x37dd0). Token 0xc5 handler for the accumulation operation. Reads an operation code and a value; sets an invalid-operation error if the state-block byte at offset 0x1d4 is zero and an invalid-enum error for codes outside 0x100-0x104; returns quietly unless the render mode is 0x1c00. Otherwise it writes raster registers through descriptor 0x584fc800, sends the operation through port 0x474, covers the rectangle with the rectangle generator and, for the two-pass operations, repeats once. |
| `generate_rect` | 0x0c037fb8 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x37fb8). Programs the raster boards to cover the current rectangle: packs four 16-bit values, stored as byte pairs at state-block offsets 0x87a-0x881, into two words, sends the first with the shared raster-bus word writer and writes the second to raster-bus register 0x150c. Its only caller is the accumulation handler. |

## Pixel path: video sources

Pixel transfers fed from the video input.

| Symbol | Address | What it is and does |
|---|---|---|
| `videoCopyPixels` | 0x0c02bd60 | **Established** (`arm1.u` +0x2bd60).
